```python
"""Mux video and audio into an MPEG program stream, and build the ISO."""

from pathlib import Path

from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import DvdPlan


def first_pts_command(m2v: Path) -> list[str]:
    """The first displayed frame of the raw stream, with the timestamps the mux will use."""
    return [
        "ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-fflags", "+genpts", "-select_streams", "v:0",
        "-show_entries", "frame=pts_time", "-read_intervals", "%+#1", "-of", "csv=p=0",
        str(m2v),
    ]  # fmt: skip


def parse_first_pts(output: str) -> float:
    lines = [line for line in output.split() if line]
    if not lines:
        raise ValueError("ffprobe returned no frame")
    return float(lines[0])


def audio_path(job_dir: Path, number: int) -> Path:
    return job_dir / f"audio{number}.ac3"


def mux_command(plan: DvdPlan, job_dir: Path, first_pts: float) -> list[str]:
    """Video and AC-3 tracks into movie.mpg.

    +genpts: without it ffmpeg writes only two nav packs and every chapter falls at 0.
    -itsoffset: the raw video starts after the audio (preload and B-frame delay, 39 to 55 ms);
    each audio is shifted to the first video frame plus its own delay in the source.
    """
    command = ["ffmpeg", "-y", "-nostdin", "-fflags", "+genpts", "-i", str(job_dir / "video.m2v")]
    for number, audio in enumerate(plan.audio, start=1):
        offset = first_pts + max(audio.offset, 0.0)
        command += ["-itsoffset", f"{offset:.6f}", "-i", str(audio_path(job_dir, number))]
    for number in range(len(plan.audio) + 1):
        command += ["-map", str(number)]
    return [
        *command, "-c", "copy", "-f", "dvd", "-muxrate", "10080k", "-packetsize", "2048",
        "-progress", "pipe:1", "-nostats", str(job_dir / "movie.mpg"),
    ]  # fmt: skip


def iso_command(plan: DvdPlan, job_dir: Path) -> list[str]:
    """genisoimage, because xorriso does not write the UDF 1.02 bridge DVD-Video needs."""
    return [
        "genisoimage", "-quiet", "-dvd-video", "-udf", "-V", plan.volume,
        "-o", str(job_dir / "disc.iso"), str(job_dir / "dvd"),
    ]  # fmt: skip
```

`src/projeto_dvd/authoring/dvd/dvdauthor.py`:

```python
"""dvdauthor XML for a disc without menu: the title starts as soon as the disc is inserted."""

import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from pathlib import Path

from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import DvdPlan

SHOW_FIRST_SUBTITLE = 64  # bit 6 = display, plus track 0


def chapter_time(seconds: float) -> str:
    hours, rest = divmod(seconds, 3600)
    minutes, secs = divmod(rest, 60)
    return f"{int(hours)}:{int(minutes):02d}:{secs:06.3f}"


def dvdauthor_xml(plan: DvdPlan, job_dir: Path) -> str:
    root = ET.Element("dvdauthor", dest=str(job_dir / "dvd"))
    vmgm = ET.SubElement(root, "vmgm")
    ET.SubElement(vmgm, "fpc").text = "jump title 1;"
    # Without a video format here dvdauthor fails with "no video format specified for VMGM".
    ET.SubElement(ET.SubElement(vmgm, "menus"), "video", format="ntsc", aspect=plan.aspect)
    titles = ET.SubElement(ET.SubElement(root, "titleset"), "titles")
    video = {"format": "ntsc", "aspect": plan.aspect}
    if plan.aspect == "16:9":
        video["widescreen"] = "nopanscan"  # a 4:3 TV letterboxes instead of cropping
    ET.SubElement(titles, "video", video)
    for audio in plan.audio:
        ET.SubElement(titles, "audio", {"lang": audio.language} if audio.language else {})
    for subtitle in plan.subtitles:
        attrs = {"lang": subtitle.language} if subtitle.language else {}
        ET.SubElement(titles, "subpicture", attrs)
    pgc = ET.SubElement(titles, "pgc")
    # audio=0 always: otherwise the player picks by its own language setting (often English).
    subtitle = SHOW_FIRST_SUBTITLE if plan.subtitle_on else 0
    ET.SubElement(pgc, "pre").text = f"audio=0;subtitle={subtitle};"
    chapters = ",".join(chapter_time(start) for start in plan.chapters)
    ET.SubElement(pgc, "vob", file=str(job_dir / "movie.mpg"), chapters=chapters)
    ET.SubElement(pgc, "post").text = "exit;"
    return ET.tostring(root, encoding="unicode")


def dvdauthor_command(xml: Path) -> list[str]:
    return ["dvdauthor", "-x", str(xml)]
```

### Task 8: Process runner (progress, cancel, kill, log)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/projeto_dvd/jobs/__init__.py`, `src/projeto_dvd/jobs/runner.py`
- Test: `tests/unit/test_runner.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `Canceled(Exception)`; `ToolError(Exception)` with `tool: str`, `returncode: int`, `stderr: str` (tail).
  - `RunResult(progress: dict[str, str], stderr: str)`.
  - `parse_progress(lines: Iterable[str]) -> Iterator[dict[str, str]]` (a block per `progress=` line).
  - `progress_fraction(block: dict[str, str], duration: float | None) -> float | None`.
  - `ProcessRunner(log_path: Path, should_cancel: Callable[[], bool], cwd: Path | None = None, poll_seconds: float = 1.0, terminate_seconds: float = 3.0)` with
    `run(command: list[str], *, duration: float | None = None, on_progress: Callable[[float], None] | None = None, stdin: Path | None = None, stdout: Path | None = None) -> RunResult` and
    `capture(command: list[str]) -> str`.
  - `Tools` protocol (same two methods), used by the pipeline.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (`tests/unit/test_runner.py`; real cheap processes,
  `sys.executable -c`, no media tools)

```python
import os
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from projeto_dvd.jobs.runner import (
    Canceled,
    ProcessRunner,
    ToolError,
    parse_progress,
    progress_fraction,
)

PY = sys.executable


def runner(tmp_path: Path, cancel: list[bool] | None = None) -> ProcessRunner:
    flag = cancel if cancel is not None else [False]
    return ProcessRunner(log_path=tmp_path / "job.log", should_cancel=lambda: flag[0],
                         cwd=tmp_path, poll_seconds=0.05, terminate_seconds=0.5)  # fmt: skip


def test_parse_progress_blocks() -> None:
    lines = ["frame=10\n", "out_time_us=N/A\n", "progress=continue\n",
             "out_time_us=5000000\n", "dup_frames=0\n", "progress=end\n"]  # fmt: skip

    blocks = list(parse_progress(lines))

    assert blocks == [
        {"frame": "10", "out_time_us": "N/A", "progress": "continue"},
        {"out_time_us": "5000000", "dup_frames": "0", "progress": "end"},
    ]


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("block", "duration", "fraction"),
    [
        ({"out_time_us": "5000000", "progress": "continue"}, 10.0, 0.5),
        ({"out_time_us": "N/A", "progress": "continue"}, 10.0, None),
        ({"out_time_us": "20000000", "progress": "continue"}, 10.0, 1.0),
        ({"progress": "end"}, 10.0, 1.0),
        ({"out_time_us": "5000000", "progress": "continue"}, None, None),
    ],
)
def test_progress_fraction(block: dict[str, str], duration: float | None,
                           fraction: float | None) -> None:  # fmt: skip
    assert progress_fraction(block, duration) == fraction


def test_run_reports_progress_and_returns_the_last_block(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    script = "print('out_time_us=1000000\\nprogress=continue\\nout_time_us=2000000\\n" \
             "dup_frames=0\\nprogress=end', flush=True)"  # fmt: skip
    seen: list[float] = []

    result = runner(tmp_path).run([PY, "-c", script], duration=2.0, on_progress=seen.append)

    assert seen == [0.5, 1.0]
    assert result.progress["dup_frames"] == "0"


def test_stderr_goes_to_the_log_and_never_blocks(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    script = "import sys; sys.stderr.write('x' * 5_000_000 + '\\nbuffer underflow\\n')"

    result = runner(tmp_path).run([PY, "-c", script])

    assert "buffer underflow" in result.stderr
    log = (tmp_path / "job.log").read_text()
    assert log.startswith("$ ") and "buffer underflow" in log


def test_failing_tool_raises_with_the_stderr_tail(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    script = "import sys; sys.stderr.write('No space left on device\\n'); sys.exit(3)"

    with pytest.raises(ToolError) as caught:
        runner(tmp_path).run([PY, "-c", script])

    assert caught.value.returncode == 3
    assert "No space left" in caught.value.stderr


def test_cancel_terminates_the_process(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    pid_file = tmp_path / "pid"
    script = f"import os, time; open({str(pid_file)!r}, 'w').write(str(os.getpid())); " \
             "time.sleep(60)"  # fmt: skip
    cancel = [False]
    run = runner(tmp_path, cancel)

    def cancel_when_started() -> bool:
        cancel[0] = pid_file.exists()
        return cancel[0]

    run.should_cancel = cancel_when_started
    with pytest.raises(Canceled):
        run.run([PY, "-c", script])

    with pytest.raises(ProcessLookupError):
        os.kill(int(pid_file.read_text()), 0)


def test_cancel_kills_a_process_that_ignores_sigterm(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    pid_file = tmp_path / "pid"
    script = ("import os, signal, time; signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIG_IGN); "
              f"open({str(pid_file)!r}, 'w').write(str(os.getpid())); time.sleep(60)")  # fmt: skip
    run = runner(tmp_path)
    run.should_cancel = pid_file.exists
    started = time.monotonic()

    with pytest.raises(Canceled):
        run.run([PY, "-c", script])

    assert time.monotonic() - started < 10
    with pytest.raises(ProcessLookupError):
        os.kill(int(pid_file.read_text()), 0)


def test_canceled_before_start_runs_nothing(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(Canceled):
        runner(tmp_path, [True]).run([PY, "-c", "open('ran', 'w')"])

    assert not (tmp_path / "ran").exists()


def test_stdin_and_stdout_files(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "in.txt").write_text("hello")
    script = "import sys; sys.stdout.write(sys.stdin.read().upper())"

    runner(tmp_path).run([PY, "-c", script], stdin=tmp_path / "in.txt",
                         stdout=tmp_path / "out.txt")  # fmt: skip

    assert (tmp_path / "out.txt").read_text() == "HELLO"


def test_capture_returns_stdout(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    assert runner(tmp_path).capture([PY, "-c", "print('0.055')"]).strip() == "0.055"


def test_capture_failure_raises(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ToolError):
        runner(tmp_path).capture([PY, "-c", "import sys; sys.exit(1)"])


def test_missing_tool_is_a_tool_error(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ToolError, match="not found"):
        runner(tmp_path).run(["/nonexistent/tool"])
```

`src/projeto_dvd/jobs/runner.py`:

```python
"""Run the media tools: progress from -progress, stderr to the job log, cancel and kill.

stderr goes straight to the log file (never a pipe, which would block the tool once its
buffer fills); only the -progress lines on stdout are read.
"""

import shlex
import subprocess
import threading
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable, Iterator
from contextlib import ExitStack
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import IO, Protocol

STDERR_KEEP = 1_000_000  # bytes of stderr kept in a RunResult
ERROR_TAIL = 2000
CAPTURE_TIMEOUT = 600


class Canceled(Exception):
    """The job was canceled, or the worker is stopping."""


class ToolError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, tool: str, returncode: int, stderr: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"{tool} exited with {returncode}")
        self.tool = tool
        self.returncode = returncode
        self.stderr = stderr[-ERROR_TAIL:]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RunResult:
    progress: dict[str, str]  # the last -progress block
    stderr: str  # what the tool wrote to stderr in this run


class Tools(Protocol):
    def run(
        self,
        command: list[str],
        *,
        duration: float | None = None,
        on_progress: Callable[[float], None] | None = None,
        stdin: Path | None = None,
        stdout: Path | None = None,
    ) -> RunResult: ...

    def capture(self, command: list[str]) -> str: ...


def parse_progress(lines: Iterable[str]) -> Iterator[dict[str, str]]:
    block: dict[str, str] = {}
    for line in lines:
        key, _, value = line.strip().partition("=")
        if not key:
            continue
        block[key] = value
        if key == "progress":
            yield block
            block = {}


def progress_fraction(block: dict[str, str], duration: float | None) -> float | None:
    if block.get("progress") == "end":
        return 1.0
    microseconds = block.get("out_time_us", "")
    if not duration or not microseconds.isdecimal():
        return None
    return min(1.0, int(microseconds) / 1e6 / duration)


@dataclass
class ProcessRunner:
    log_path: Path
    should_cancel: Callable[[], bool]
    cwd: Path | None = None
    poll_seconds: float = 1.0
    terminate_seconds: float = 3.0

    def run(
        self,
        command: list[str],
        *,
        duration: float | None = None,
        on_progress: Callable[[float], None] | None = None,
        stdin: Path | None = None,
        stdout: Path | None = None,
    ) -> RunResult:
        if self.should_cancel():
            raise Canceled
        blocks: list[dict[str, str]] = []
        with ExitStack() as stack:
            log = stack.enter_context(self.log_path.open("ab"))
            log.write(f"$ {shlex.join(command)}\n".encode())
            log.flush()
            start = log.tell()
            source: IO[bytes] | int = (
                stack.enter_context(stdin.open("rb")) if stdin else subprocess.DEVNULL
            )
            target: IO[bytes] | int = (
                stack.enter_context(stdout.open("wb")) if stdout else subprocess.PIPE
            )
            try:
                process = subprocess.Popen(
                    command, stdin=source, stdout=target, stderr=log, cwd=self.cwd
                )
            except OSError as error:
                raise ToolError(command[0], -1, f"{command[0]} not found: {error}") from error
            reader = None
            if process.stdout is not None:
                reader = threading.Thread(
                    target=self._read, args=(process.stdout, duration, on_progress, blocks),
                    daemon=True,
                )  # fmt: skip
                reader.start()
            try:
                self._wait(process)
            finally:
                if reader is not None:
                    reader.join()
        stderr = self._stderr_since(start)
        if process.returncode != 0:
            raise ToolError(command[0], process.returncode, stderr)
        return RunResult(blocks[-1] if blocks else {}, stderr)

    def capture(self, command: list[str]) -> str:
        with self.log_path.open("ab") as log:
            log.write(f"$ {shlex.join(command)}\n".encode())
            try:
                result = subprocess.run(
                    command, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, capture_output=True,
                    timeout=CAPTURE_TIMEOUT, cwd=self.cwd, check=False,
                )  # fmt: skip
            except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
                raise ToolError(command[0], -1, str(error)) from error
            log.write(result.stderr)
        if result.returncode != 0:
            raise ToolError(command[0], result.returncode, result.stderr.decode(errors="replace"))
        return result.stdout.decode(errors="replace")

    def _wait(self, process: subprocess.Popen[bytes]) -> None:
        while True:
            try:
                process.wait(timeout=self.poll_seconds)
                return
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                pass
            if self.should_cancel():
                # The tools start no children of their own: the process itself is enough.
                process.terminate()
                try:
                    process.wait(timeout=self.terminate_seconds)
                except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                    process.kill()
                    process.wait()
                raise Canceled

    @staticmethod
    def _read(
        stream: IO[bytes],
        duration: float | None,
        on_progress: Callable[[float], None] | None,
        blocks: list[dict[str, str]],
    ) -> None:
        lines = (line.decode(errors="replace") for line in stream)
        for block in parse_progress(lines):
            blocks.append(block)
            fraction = progress_fraction(block, duration)
            if fraction is not None and on_progress is not None:
                on_progress(fraction)

    def _stderr_since(self, start: int) -> str:
        with self.log_path.open("rb") as log:
            log.seek(max(start, log.seek(0, 2) - STDERR_KEEP))
            return log.read().decode(errors="replace")
```

**Files:**
- Create: `src/projeto_dvd/planner/disc_source.py`, `src/projeto_dvd/jobs/pipeline.py`
- Modify: `Dockerfile` (genisoimage), `src/projeto_dvd/tools.py` (genisoimage in `/health`), `.github/workflows/ci.yml` (tool count 7), `pyproject.toml` (coverage `exclude_also` for `if __name__ == "__main__":`), `.gitignore` (`*.part`, `ffmpeg2pass-*`)
- Test: `tests/unit/test_disc_source.py`, `tests/unit/test_pipeline.py`, `tests/unit/test_tools.py`, `tests/integration/clips.py`, `tests/integration/test_dvd.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1 to 8.
- Produces:
  - `disc_source.SCAN_FIRST`, `CHANGED`, `ONE_VIDEO`, `READ_ERROR` (texts of the spec).
  - `disc_source.folder_reasons(view: FolderView) -> tuple[str, ...]` (empty folder: `()`; the screen hides the block).
  - `disc_source.DiscSource(folder: str, video: VideoFile, summary: Summary, options: list[SubtitleOption], mode: VideoMode, top_field_first: bool)`
  - `disc_source.prepare(folder: str, video: VideoFile, summary: Summary, capture: Callable[[list[str]], str]) -> DiscSource | tuple[str, ...]`
  - `disc_source.run_capture(command: list[str]) -> str` (subprocess, 120 s timeout; raises `OSError`/`CalledProcessError`)
  - `disc_source.suggested_plan(source: DiscSource, **choice_overrides: Any) -> DvdPlan`
  - `pipeline.Step(StrEnum)`: `VIDEO_PASS1, VIDEO_PASS2, PULLDOWN, AUDIO, SUBTITLES, MUX, AUTHOR, ISO, QA, COPY`; `STEP_LABELS: dict[Step, str]`; `STEP_WEIGHTS: dict[Step, int]`; `overall_progress(step: Step, fraction: float) -> float`.
  - `pipeline.PipelineError(Exception)` with `message: str`, `step: Step | None`; `SOURCE_CHANGED`, `NO_SPACE`, `ISO_EXISTS` texts.
  - `pipeline.PipelineResult(iso_path: Path, qa_json: str | None)` (QA arrives in Task 11).
  - `pipeline.run_pipeline(plan: DvdPlan, *, media_dir: Path, job_dir: Path, output_dir: Path, tools: Tools, report: Callable[[Step, float], None], should_cancel: Callable[[], bool]) -> PipelineResult`
  - `pipeline.preflight(work_dir: Path, output_dir: Path, estimated_bytes: int, iso_name: str, replace: bool) -> list[str]`
  - `pipeline.main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int` (dev command).
  - `tests/integration/clips.py`: `ffmpeg(*args: str) -> None`, `movie_1080p(path: Path, seconds: int = 12) -> Path`, `plan_for(media: Path, folder: str, **overrides: Any) -> DvdPlan`.

```python
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from projeto_dvd.library.files import VideoFile
from projeto_dvd.library.probe import summarize
from projeto_dvd.library.scan import FileView, FolderView, Status
from projeto_dvd.planner import disc_source, source
from projeto_dvd.planner.disc_source import folder_reasons, prepare, suggested_plan
from projeto_dvd.planner.source import VideoMode

VIDEO = VideoFile(path="F/f.mkv", absolute=Path("/media/F/f.mkv"), size=10, mtime_ns=20)
H264 = {"index": 0, "codec_type": "video", "codec_name": "h264", "width": 1920, "height": 1080,
        "r_frame_rate": "24000/1001", "avg_frame_rate": "24000/1001",
        "display_aspect_ratio": "16:9"}  # fmt: skip
MPEG2 = {**H264, "codec_name": "mpeg2video", "width": 720, "height": 480,
         "r_frame_rate": "30000/1001", "avg_frame_rate": "30000/1001"}  # fmt: skip
AUDIO = {"index": 1, "codec_type": "audio", "codec_name": "ac3", "channels": 2,
         "sample_rate": "48000", "bit_rate": "192000", "tags": {"language": "por"}}  # fmt: skip


def summary(*streams: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
    return summarize({"streams": list(streams), "format": {"duration": "600"}})


def view(status: Status, files: tuple[FileView, ...]) -> FolderView:
    return FolderView(name="F", status=status, files=files, size=10, duration=600.0)


def test_folder_reasons() -> None:
    good = FileView(video=VIDEO, summary=summary(H264, AUDIO), error=None)

    assert folder_reasons(view(Status.EMPTY, ())) == ()
    assert folder_reasons(view(Status.NOT_SCANNED, (good,))) == (disc_source.SCAN_FIRST,)
    assert folder_reasons(view(Status.CHANGED, (good,))) == (disc_source.CHANGED,)
    assert folder_reasons(view(Status.OK, (good, good))) == (disc_source.ONE_VIDEO,)
    broken = FileView(video=VIDEO, summary=None, error="x")
    assert folder_reasons(view(Status.WARNINGS, (broken,))) == (disc_source.READ_ERROR,)
    assert folder_reasons(view(Status.OK, (good,))) == ()


def test_prepare_h264_film_without_frame_scan() -> None:
    def capture(command: list[str]) -> str:
        raise AssertionError("no frame scan for H.264")

    result = prepare("F", VIDEO, summary(H264, AUDIO), capture)

    assert isinstance(result, disc_source.DiscSource)
    assert result.mode == VideoMode.FILM


def test_prepare_mpeg2_uses_the_frames() -> None:
    frames = [{"repeat_pict": r, "interlaced_frame": 0, "top_field_first": t}
              for r, t in [(1, 1), (0, 0), (1, 0), (0, 1)] * 20]  # fmt: skip
    calls: list[list[str]] = []

    def capture(command: list[str]) -> str:
        calls.append(command)
        return json.dumps({"frames": frames})

    result = prepare("F", VIDEO, summary(MPEG2, AUDIO), capture)

    assert isinstance(result, disc_source.DiscSource)
    assert result.mode == VideoMode.FILM
    assert calls[0][0] == "ffprobe"


def test_prepare_returns_refusals() -> None:
    result = prepare("F", VIDEO, summary(AUDIO), lambda command: "")

    assert result == (source.NO_VIDEO,)


def test_prepare_turns_a_failed_frame_scan_into_a_refusal() -> None:
    def capture(command: list[str]) -> str:
        raise OSError("boom")

    assert prepare("F", VIDEO, summary(MPEG2, AUDIO), capture) == (source.FRAMES_UNREADABLE,)


def test_suggested_plan() -> None:
    result = prepare("F", VIDEO, summary(H264, AUDIO), lambda command: "")
    assert isinstance(result, disc_source.DiscSource)

    plan = suggested_plan(result)

    assert plan.profile == "dvd5"
    assert plan.audio[0].action == "copy"
    assert plan.path == "F/f.mkv"
```

`tests/unit/test_pipeline.py` (fake tools that create the files each command would create):

```python
import os
import shutil
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from projeto_dvd.jobs import pipeline
from projeto_dvd.jobs.pipeline import (
    PipelineError,
    Step,
    overall_progress,
    preflight,
    run_pipeline,
)
from projeto_dvd.jobs.runner import Canceled, RunResult, ToolError
from tests.plans import make_plan
from tests.tree import make_file


class FakeTools:
    """Records commands; creates the output file of each; can fail at one tool."""

    def __init__(self, fail_at: str | None = None, stderr: str = "") -> None:
        self.commands: list[list[str]] = []
        self.fail_at = fail_at
        self.stderr = stderr

    def run(self, command: list[str], *, duration: float | None = None,
            on_progress: Callable[[float], None] | None = None, stdin: Path | None = None,
            stdout: Path | None = None) -> RunResult:  # fmt: skip
        self.commands.append(command)
        if self.fail_at and self.fail_at in " ".join(command):
            raise ToolError(command[0], 1, self.stderr)
        if on_progress:
            on_progress(1.0)
        if stdout is not None:
            make_file(stdout, b"spu")
        elif command[0] == "dvdauthor":
            dvd = Path(command[2]).parent / "dvd" / "VIDEO_TS"
            make_file(dvd / "VTS_01_1.VOB", b"v" * 100)
        elif command[-1] != "-":
            make_file(Path(command[-1] if command[0] != "genisoimage" else command[-2]),
                      b"\x00\x00\x01\xb3" + bytes([0x2D, 0x01, 0xE0, 0x31]) + b"\xff" * 8)  # fmt: skip
        return RunResult({"dup_frames": "0", "drop_frames": "0", "progress": "end"}, "")

    def capture(self, command: list[str]) -> str:
        self.commands.append(command)
        return "0.055556\n"


@pytest.fixture
def dirs(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path, Path]:
    media, job, output = tmp_path / "media", tmp_path / "work" / "jobs" / "1", tmp_path / "out"
    source = make_file(media / "Filme" / "Filme.mkv", b"x" * 100)
    os.utime(source, ns=(200, 200))
    return media, job, output


def run(dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path], tools: FakeTools, **plan_fields: object) -> pipeline.PipelineResult:
    media, job, output = dirs
    steps: list[tuple[Step, float]] = []
    monkey_pulldown = plan_fields.pop("pulldown", None)
    return run_pipeline(make_plan(**plan_fields), media_dir=media, job_dir=job, output_dir=output,
                        tools=tools, report=lambda s, f: steps.append((s, f)),
                        should_cancel=lambda: False)  # fmt: skip


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def no_pulldown(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> list[Path]:
    patched: list[Path] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline, "apply_soft_pulldown",
                        lambda path, checkpoint: patched.append(path) or 1)  # fmt: skip
    return patched


def test_runs_the_steps_in_order_and_publishes_the_iso(
    dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path], no_pulldown: list[Path]
) -> None:
    tools = FakeTools()

    result = run(dirs, tools)

    tools_used = [command[0] for command in tools.commands]
    assert tools_used == ["ffmpeg", "ffmpeg", "ffmpeg", "ffmpeg", "ffprobe", "ffmpeg",
                          "dvdauthor", "genisoimage"]  # fmt: skip
    assert no_pulldown == [dirs[1] / "video.m2v"]
    assert result.iso_path == dirs[2] / "iso" / "Filme.iso"
    assert result.iso_path.exists()
    job = dirs[1]
    for leftover in ("video.m2v", "audio1.ac3", "audio2.ac3", "movie.mpg", "dvd", "disc.iso"):
        assert not (job / leftover).exists(), leftover
    assert (job / "dvd.xml").exists()


def test_interlaced_plan_skips_the_pulldown(
    dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path], no_pulldown: list[Path]
) -> None:
    from projeto_dvd.planner.source import VideoMode

    run(dirs, FakeTools(), mode=VideoMode.INTERLACED)

    assert no_pulldown == []


def test_changed_source_stops_before_any_tool(dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path]) -> None:
    tools = FakeTools()
    os.utime(dirs[0] / "Filme" / "Filme.mkv", ns=(999, 999))

    with pytest.raises(PipelineError) as caught:
        run(dirs, tools)

    assert caught.value.message == pipeline.SOURCE_CHANGED
    assert tools.commands == []


def test_tool_failure_names_the_step(dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path]) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(PipelineError) as caught:
        run(dirs, FakeTools(fail_at="-pass 2"))

    assert caught.value.step == Step.VIDEO_PASS2
    assert caught.value.message == "Falhou na etapa Vídeo: 2ª passada (codificação)."


def test_no_space_has_its_own_message(dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path]) -> None:
    tools = FakeTools(fail_at="dvdauthor", stderr="write failed: No space left on device")

    with pytest.raises(PipelineError) as caught:
        run(dirs, tools)

    assert caught.value.message == pipeline.NO_SPACE


def test_cancel_between_steps(dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path]) -> None:
    media, job, output = dirs
    tools = FakeTools()
    calls = iter([False, False, True])

    with pytest.raises(Canceled):
        run_pipeline(make_plan(), media_dir=media, job_dir=job, output_dir=output, tools=tools,
                     report=lambda s, f: None, should_cancel=lambda: next(calls, True))  # fmt: skip

    assert len(tools.commands) == 2


def test_existing_iso_is_not_replaced_without_confirmation(dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path]) -> None:
    make_file(dirs[2] / "iso" / "Filme.iso", b"old")

    with pytest.raises(PipelineError) as caught:
        run(dirs, FakeTools())

    assert caught.value.message == pipeline.ISO_EXISTS
    assert (dirs[2] / "iso" / "Filme.iso").read_bytes() == b"old"
    assert not (dirs[2] / "iso" / "Filme.iso.part").exists()
    assert (dirs[1] / "disc.iso").exists()  # kept for inspection


def test_existing_iso_is_replaced_when_confirmed(dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path]) -> None:
    make_file(dirs[2] / "iso" / "Filme.iso", b"old")

    result = run(dirs, FakeTools(), replace_existing=True)

    assert result.iso_path.read_bytes() != b"old"


def test_failed_copy_removes_the_part_file(
    dirs: tuple[Path, Path, Path], monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    def broken_replace(source: object, target: object) -> None:
        raise OSError(28, "No space left on device")

    monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline.os, "replace", broken_replace)

    with pytest.raises(PipelineError):
        run(dirs, FakeTools())

    assert not (dirs[2] / "iso" / "Filme.iso.part").exists()
    assert (dirs[1] / "disc.iso").exists()


def test_overall_progress_uses_the_weights() -> None:
    assert overall_progress(Step.VIDEO_PASS1, 0.0) == 0.0
    assert overall_progress(Step.VIDEO_PASS2, 0.0) == pytest.approx(35 / 102)
    assert overall_progress(Step.COPY, 1.0) == 1.0


def test_preflight_reports_space_and_existing_iso(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    work, output = tmp_path / "work", tmp_path / "out"
    work.mkdir()
    make_file(output / "iso" / "Filme.iso")
    usage = shutil.disk_usage(tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline.shutil, "disk_usage",
                        lambda path: usage._replace(free=12_000_000_000))  # fmt: skip

    errors = preflight(work, output, 8_500_000_000, "Filme.iso", replace=False)

    assert "Falta espaço na pasta de trabalho: precisa de 18,1 GB livres e há 12 GB." in errors
    assert pipeline.ISO_EXISTS_FORM in errors
    assert preflight(work, output, 1_000_000, "Filme.iso", replace=True) == []


def test_preflight_reports_a_folder_that_cannot_be_written(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    errors = preflight(tmp_path / "missing", tmp_path, 1, "F.iso", replace=False)

    assert errors == [pipeline.cannot_write("pasta de trabalho", tmp_path / "missing")]
```

```python
"""The one video of a disc folder, ready to plan: shared by the web form and the dev command."""

import subprocess
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from projeto_dvd.library.files import VideoFile
from projeto_dvd.library.probe import Summary
from projeto_dvd.library.scan import FolderView, Status
from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import (
    DvdPlan,
    SubtitleOption,
    build_plan,
    subtitle_options,
    suggest_choices,
)
from projeto_dvd.planner.source import (
    FRAMES_UNREADABLE,
    VideoMode,
    check_video,
    classify_frames,
    frame_scan_command,
    top_field_first_from_frames,
)

SCAN_FIRST = "Escaneie a pasta antes de gerar o disco."
CHANGED = "Os arquivos mudaram desde o último scan. Escaneie de novo."
ONE_VIDEO = (
    "Por enquanto o app gera disco com um vídeo só (discos com vários vídeos chegam com o "
    "menu). Para gerar agora, deixe este vídeo sozinho numa pasta."
)
READ_ERROR = (
    "Não foi possível ler o arquivo. Escaneie de novo; se continuar, o arquivo pode estar "
    "danificado."
)
FRAME_SCAN_TIMEOUT = 120


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiscSource:
    folder: str
    video: VideoFile
    summary: Summary
    options: list[SubtitleOption]
    mode: VideoMode
    top_field_first: bool


def folder_reasons(view: FolderView) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Folder-level reasons; an empty folder has none (the screen shows no DVD block)."""
    if view.status == Status.EMPTY:
        return ()
    if view.status == Status.NOT_SCANNED:
        return (SCAN_FIRST,)
    if view.status == Status.CHANGED:
        return (CHANGED,)
    if len(view.files) != 1:
        return (ONE_VIDEO,)
    file = view.files[0]
    if file.error is not None:
        return (READ_ERROR,)
    if file.summary is None:
        return (SCAN_FIRST,)
    return check_video(file.summary).reasons


def prepare(
    folder: str, video: VideoFile, summary: Summary, capture: Callable[[list[str]], str]
) -> DiscSource | tuple[str, ...]:
    check = check_video(summary)
    if check.reasons:
        return check.reasons
    mode, top_field_first = check.mode, check.top_field_first
    if check.needs_frame_scan:
        try:
            frames = capture(frame_scan_command(video.absolute, summary.duration or 0.0))
        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
            return (FRAMES_UNREADABLE,)
        result = classify_frames(frames)
        if isinstance(result, str):
            return (result,)
        mode, top_field_first = result, top_field_first_from_frames(frames)
    assert mode is not None
    options = subtitle_options(summary, video.subtitles)
    return DiscSource(folder, video, summary, options, mode, top_field_first)


def run_capture(command: list[str]) -> str:
    result = subprocess.run(
        command, capture_output=True, text=True, errors="replace",
        timeout=FRAME_SCAN_TIMEOUT, check=True,
    )  # fmt: skip
    return result.stdout


def suggested_plan(source: DiscSource, **choice_overrides: Any) -> DvdPlan:
    duration = source.summary.duration or 0.0
    choices = suggest_choices(source.summary, source.options, duration)
    if choice_overrides:
        choices = choices.model_copy(update=choice_overrides)
    return build_plan(
        folder=source.folder, path=source.video.path, size=source.video.size,
        mtime_ns=source.video.mtime_ns, summary=source.summary, options=source.options,
        choices=choices, mode=source.mode, top_field_first=source.top_field_first,
    )  # fmt: skip
```

  (`prepare` uses `assert mode is not None` only as a type narrow: `check_video` always sets a
  mode when there are no reasons and no frame scan. If mypy accepts a cleaner narrow, prefer
  it; an `assert` in production code is acceptable here because it documents an invariant.)

`src/projeto_dvd/jobs/pipeline.py`:

```python
"""From a DvdPlan to an ISO in the output folder: encode, mux, author, check, publish.

Intermediate files are deleted as soon as the next step succeeds, so the work folder peaks at
about twice the disc size. The ISO reaches the output folder only after the automatic QA.
"""

import argparse
import os
import shutil
import sys
import tempfile
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum
from pathlib import Path

from sqlmodel import Session

from projeto_dvd.api.formatting import format_size
from projeto_dvd.authoring.dvd.dvdauthor import dvdauthor_command, dvdauthor_xml
from projeto_dvd.authoring.dvd.mux import (
    audio_path,
    first_pts_command,
    iso_command,
    mux_command,
    parse_first_pts,
)
from projeto_dvd.config import Settings
from projeto_dvd.db.session import make_engine, migrate
from projeto_dvd.encode.audio import audio_command
from projeto_dvd.encode.mpeg2 import PulldownError, apply_soft_pulldown
from projeto_dvd.encode.video import video_pass_command
from projeto_dvd.jobs.runner import Canceled, ProcessRunner, ToolError, Tools
from projeto_dvd.library.files import list_disc_folders
from projeto_dvd.library.probe import run_ffprobe
from projeto_dvd.library.scan import folder_view, scan_folder
from projeto_dvd.planner.disc_source import folder_reasons, prepare, run_capture, suggested_plan
from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import DvdPlan
from projeto_dvd.planner.source import VideoMode

COPY_CHUNK = 16 * 1024 * 1024
GIB = 1024**3
SOURCE_CHANGED = (
    "O vídeo mudou ou ficou inacessível depois que o job foi criado (o HD externo foi "
    "desconectado?). Escaneie a pasta e gere de novo."
)
NO_SPACE = (
    "Acabou o espaço na pasta de trabalho. Libere espaço ou apague os arquivos temporários de "
    "outros jobs."
)
NO_SPACE_OUTPUT = "Acabou o espaço na pasta de saída ao copiar o ISO."
ISO_EXISTS = (
    "Já existe um ISO com este nome na pasta de saída, e a substituição não foi confirmada."
)
ISO_EXISTS_FORM = "Já existe um ISO com este nome. Marque \"Substituir o ISO existente\"."


class Step(StrEnum):
    VIDEO_PASS1 = "video_pass1"
    VIDEO_PASS2 = "video_pass2"
    PULLDOWN = "pulldown"
    AUDIO = "audio"
    SUBTITLES = "subtitles"
    MUX = "mux"
    AUTHOR = "author"
    ISO = "iso"
    QA = "qa"
    COPY = "copy"


STEP_LABELS = {
    Step.VIDEO_PASS1: "Vídeo: 1ª passada (análise)",
    Step.VIDEO_PASS2: "Vídeo: 2ª passada (codificação)",
    Step.PULLDOWN: "Ajuste de quadros para TV",
    Step.AUDIO: "Áudio",
    Step.SUBTITLES: "Legendas",
    Step.MUX: "Juntando vídeo, áudio e legendas",
    Step.AUTHOR: "Montando a estrutura do DVD",
    Step.ISO: "Criando o ISO",
    Step.QA: "Conferindo o disco",
    Step.COPY: "Copiando o ISO para a pasta de saída",
}
STEP_WEIGHTS = {
    Step.VIDEO_PASS1: 35, Step.VIDEO_PASS2: 45, Step.PULLDOWN: 2, Step.AUDIO: 5,
    Step.SUBTITLES: 2, Step.MUX: 4, Step.AUTHOR: 2, Step.ISO: 2, Step.QA: 3, Step.COPY: 2,
}  # fmt: skip
TOTAL_WEIGHT = sum(STEP_WEIGHTS.values())


class PipelineError(Exception):
    """A failure with a Portuguese message for the job page."""

    def __init__(self, message: str, step: Step | None = None) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.message = message
        self.step = step


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PipelineResult:
    iso_path: Path
    qa_json: str | None


def overall_progress(step: Step, fraction: float) -> float:
    done = 0
    for item in Step:
        if item == step:
            break
        done += STEP_WEIGHTS[item]
    return (done + STEP_WEIGHTS[step] * fraction) / TOTAL_WEIGHT


def run_pipeline(
    plan: DvdPlan,
    *,
    media_dir: Path,
    job_dir: Path,
    output_dir: Path,
    tools: Tools,
    report: Callable[[Step, float], None],
    should_cancel: Callable[[], bool],
) -> PipelineResult:
    source = media_dir / plan.path
    _check_source(source, plan)
    job_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    def begin(step: Step) -> Callable[[float], None]:
        if should_cancel():
            raise Canceled
        report(step, 0.0)
        return lambda fraction: report(step, fraction)

    def checkpoint() -> None:
        if should_cancel():
            raise Canceled

    m2v = job_dir / "video.m2v"
    for number, step in ((1, Step.VIDEO_PASS1), (2, Step.VIDEO_PASS2)):
        progress = begin(step)
        with _tool_step(step):
            tools.run(
                video_pass_command(plan, source, job_dir, 1 if number == 1 else 2),
                duration=plan.duration, on_progress=progress,
            )  # fmt: skip
    for log in job_dir.glob("pass-*.log*"):
        log.unlink()
    if plan.mode == VideoMode.FILM:
        begin(Step.PULLDOWN)
        try:
            apply_soft_pulldown(m2v, checkpoint)
        except PulldownError as error:
            raise PipelineError(str(error), Step.PULLDOWN) from error
    progress = begin(Step.AUDIO)
    for number, audio in enumerate(plan.audio, start=1):
        with _tool_step(Step.AUDIO):
            tools.run(audio_command(audio, source, audio_path(job_dir, number)))
        progress(number / len(plan.audio))
    progress = begin(Step.MUX)
    movie = job_dir / "movie.mpg"
    with _tool_step(Step.MUX):
        first_pts = parse_first_pts(tools.capture(first_pts_command(m2v)))
        tools.run(mux_command(plan, job_dir, first_pts), duration=plan.duration,
                  on_progress=progress)  # fmt: skip
    m2v.unlink()
    for number in range(1, len(plan.audio) + 1):
        audio_path(job_dir, number).unlink()
    begin(Step.AUTHOR)
    xml = job_dir / "dvd.xml"
    xml.write_text(dvdauthor_xml(plan, job_dir), encoding="utf-8")
    shutil.rmtree(job_dir / "dvd", ignore_errors=True)  # dvdauthor appends to what exists
    with _tool_step(Step.AUTHOR):
        tools.run(dvdauthor_command(xml))
    movie.unlink()
    begin(Step.ISO)
    with _tool_step(Step.ISO):
        tools.run(iso_command(plan, job_dir))
    shutil.rmtree(job_dir / "dvd")
    iso = job_dir / "disc.iso"
    iso_path = _publish(iso, output_dir, plan, begin(Step.COPY), checkpoint)
    return PipelineResult(iso_path=iso_path, qa_json=None)


def preflight(
    work_dir: Path, output_dir: Path, estimated_bytes: int, iso_name: str, replace: bool
) -> list[str]:
    """Problems to show on the form before a job is created."""
    errors = []
    for label, folder in (("pasta de trabalho", work_dir), ("pasta de saída", output_dir)):
        if not _writable(folder):
            errors.append(cannot_write(label, folder))
    if errors:
        return errors
    for label, folder, needed in (
        ("pasta de trabalho", work_dir, 2 * estimated_bytes + GIB),
        ("pasta de saída", output_dir, estimated_bytes + 100 * 1024 * 1024),
    ):
        free = shutil.disk_usage(folder).free
        if free < needed:
            errors.append(
                f"Falta espaço na {label}: precisa de {format_size(needed)} livres e há "
                f"{format_size(free)}."
            )
    if (output_dir / "iso" / iso_name).exists() and not replace:
        errors.append(ISO_EXISTS_FORM)
    return errors


def cannot_write(label: str, folder: Path) -> str:
    return (
        f"Não dá para gravar na {label} ({folder}). Ela pode ter sido criada pelo Docker como "
        "root. Para corrigir, no WSL: sudo chown 1000:1000 <a pasta correspondente do .env>."
    )


def _check_source(source: Path, plan: DvdPlan) -> None:
    try:
        stat = source.stat()
    except OSError as error:
        raise PipelineError(SOURCE_CHANGED) from error
    if (stat.st_size, stat.st_mtime_ns) != (plan.size, plan.mtime_ns):
        raise PipelineError(SOURCE_CHANGED)


@contextmanager
def _tool_step(step: Step) -> Iterator[None]:
    try:
        yield
    except ToolError as error:
        if "No space left" in error.stderr:
            raise PipelineError(NO_SPACE, step) from error
        raise PipelineError(f"Falhou na etapa {STEP_LABELS[step]}.", step) from error
    except ValueError as error:  # unreadable tool output (first pts)
        raise PipelineError(f"Falhou na etapa {STEP_LABELS[step]}.", step) from error


def _publish(
    iso: Path,
    output_dir: Path,
    plan: DvdPlan,
    progress: Callable[[float], None],
    checkpoint: Callable[[], None],
) -> Path:
    """Copy as .part and rename at the end: a half-copied ISO never has the final name."""
    target = output_dir / "iso" / plan.iso_name
    part = target.with_name(target.name + ".part")
    try:
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if target.exists() and not plan.replace_existing:
            raise PipelineError(ISO_EXISTS, Step.COPY)
        total, copied = iso.stat().st_size, 0
        with iso.open("rb") as source, part.open("wb") as copy:
            while chunk := source.read(COPY_CHUNK):
                checkpoint()
                copy.write(chunk)
                copied += len(chunk)
                progress(copied / total)
        if target.exists() and not plan.replace_existing:  # appeared during the copy
            raise PipelineError(ISO_EXISTS, Step.COPY)
        os.replace(part, target)
    except OSError as error:
        part.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise PipelineError(NO_SPACE_OUTPUT, Step.COPY) from error
    except BaseException:
        part.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise
    iso.unlink()
    return target


def _writable(folder: Path) -> bool:
    try:
        with tempfile.TemporaryFile(dir=folder):
            return True
    except OSError:
        return False


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """Development command: scan a folder and generate its DVD with the suggested choices."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        prog="python -m projeto_dvd.jobs.pipeline",
        description="Generate the DVD ISO of one disc folder with the suggested choices.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("folder", help="disc folder name inside MEDIA_DIR")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    settings = Settings()
    folder = list_disc_folders(settings.media_dir).get(args.folder)
    if folder is None:
        print(f"Pasta não encontrada: {args.folder}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    migrate(settings.database_url)
    engine = make_engine(settings.database_url)
    with Session(engine) as session:
        scan_folder(session, settings.media_dir, args.folder, folder, run_ffprobe)
        view = folder_view(session, settings.media_dir, args.folder, folder)
    engine.dispose()
    reasons = folder_reasons(view)
    file = view.files[0] if len(view.files) == 1 else None
    if reasons or file is None or file.summary is None:
        print("\n".join(reasons or ("Nada para gerar.",)), file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    source = prepare(args.folder, file.video, file.summary, run_capture)
    if isinstance(source, tuple):
        print("\n".join(source), file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    plan = suggested_plan(source, confirm_low=True)
    job_dir = settings.work_dir / "dev" / datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")
    job_dir.mkdir(parents=True)
    runner = ProcessRunner(log_path=job_dir / "job.log", should_cancel=lambda: False, cwd=job_dir)

    def report(step: Step, fraction: float) -> None:
        print(f"\r{STEP_LABELS[step]}: {fraction:.0%}   ", end="", flush=True)

    try:
        result = run_pipeline(plan, media_dir=settings.media_dir, job_dir=job_dir,
                              output_dir=settings.output_dir, tools=runner, report=report,
                              should_cancel=lambda: False)  # fmt: skip
    except PipelineError as error:
        print(f"\n{error.message}\nLog: {job_dir / 'job.log'}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    print(f"\nISO: {result.iso_path}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

```python
def test_main_refuses_an_unknown_folder(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,

        seen.append(plan)
        kwargs["report"](Step.VIDEO_PASS1, 0.5)  # type: ignore[operator]
        return pipeline.PipelineResult(iso_path=tmp_path / "out" / "iso" / "Filme.iso", qa_json=None)

    monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline, "run_pipeline", fake_run)

    assert pipeline.main(["Filme"]) == 0
    assert seen[0].folder == "Filme"
    assert "Filme.iso" in capsys.readouterr().out
```

`tests/integration/clips.py`:

```python
"""Short lavfi clips and plans for the slow DVD tests (real tools, inside the image)."""

import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from projeto_dvd.library.files import find_videos
from projeto_dvd.library.probe import run_ffprobe, summarize
from projeto_dvd.planner.disc_source import DiscSource, prepare, run_capture, suggested_plan
from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import DvdPlan


def ffmpeg(*args: str) -> None:
    subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-nostdin", "-v", "error", "-y", *args], check=True)


def movie_1080p(path: Path, seconds: int = 12) -> Path:
    """Scope 1080p film: AAC 5.1 at 44.1 kHz (por), AC-3 2.0 192k starting 0.2 s late (eng),
    chapters at 0, 4 and 8 s."""
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    chapters = path.with_name("chapters.txt")
    chapters.write_text(
        ";FFMETADATA1\n"
        + "".join(
            f"[CHAPTER]\nTIMEBASE=1/1000\nSTART={s * 1000}\nEND={(s + 4) * 1000}\ntitle=C{s}\n"
            for s in (0, 4, 8)
        )
    )
    ffmpeg(
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"testsrc2=s=1920x800:r=24000/1001:d={seconds}",
        "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"sine=f=440:d={seconds}:sample_rate=44100",
        "-itsoffset", "0.2", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"sine=f=660:d={seconds}:sample_rate=48000",
        "-i", str(chapters),
        "-map", "0:v", "-map", "1:a", "-map", "2:a", "-map_chapters", "3",
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-preset", "ultrafast", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p",
        "-c:a:0", "aac", "-ac:a:0", "6", "-c:a:1", "ac3", "-b:a:1", "192k", "-ac:a:1", "2",
        "-metadata:s:a:0", "language=por", "-metadata:s:a:1", "language=eng",
        str(path),
    )  # fmt: skip
    chapters.unlink()
    return path


def disc_source(media: Path, folder: str) -> DiscSource:
    video = find_videos(media, media / folder)[0]
    probe = run_ffprobe(video.absolute)
    assert probe.json is not None, probe.error
    result = prepare(folder, video, summarize(json.loads(probe.json)), run_capture)
    assert isinstance(result, DiscSource), result
    return result


def plan_for(media: Path, folder: str, **overrides: Any) -> DvdPlan:
    return suggested_plan(disc_source(media, folder), **overrides)
```

`tests/integration/test_dvd.py`:

```python
"""The DVD pipeline with the real tools: run inside the Docker image (make test-slow)."""

import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from projeto_dvd.jobs.pipeline import run_pipeline
from projeto_dvd.jobs.runner import ProcessRunner
from tests.integration import clips

pytestmark = pytest.mark.slow


def generate(media: Path, folder: str, tmp_path: Path, **overrides: object) -> Path:
    plan = clips.plan_for(media, folder, **overrides)
    job = tmp_path / "work" / "jobs" / "1"
    job.mkdir(parents=True)
    runner = ProcessRunner(log_path=job / "job.log", should_cancel=lambda: False, cwd=job)
    result = run_pipeline(plan, media_dir=media, job_dir=job, output_dir=tmp_path / "out",
                          tools=runner, report=lambda step, fraction: None,
                          should_cancel=lambda: False)  # fmt: skip
    return result.iso_path


def dvd_probe(iso: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
    output = subprocess.run(
        ["ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-f", "dvdvideo", "-title", "1", "-show_streams",
         "-show_chapters", "-show_format", "-of", "json", str(iso)],
        capture_output=True, text=True, check=True,
    ).stdout  # fmt: skip
    data: dict[str, object] = json.loads(output)
    return data


def test_1080p_movie_becomes_a_dvd_iso(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    media = tmp_path / "media"
    clips.movie_1080p(media / "Filme" / "Filme.mkv")

    iso = generate(media, "Filme", tmp_path, subtitles=(), subtitle_on=None)

    assert iso == tmp_path / "out" / "iso" / "Filme.iso"
    streams = dvd_probe(iso)["streams"]
    assert isinstance(streams, list)
    video = streams[0]
    assert (video["codec_name"], video["width"], video["height"]) == ("mpeg2video", 720, 480)
    assert video["r_frame_rate"] == "30000/1001"
    assert [s["codec_name"] for s in streams[1:]] == ["ac3", "ac3"]
    leftovers = sorted(p.name for p in (tmp_path / "work" / "jobs" / "1").iterdir())
    assert leftovers == ["dvd.xml", "job.log"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `src/projeto_dvd/authoring/dvd/subtitles.py`

```python
"""Text subtitles (SRT) drawn as DVD subpictures by spumux."""

import codecs
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from pathlib import Path

FONT = Path("/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf")
TIMING = re.compile(
    r"^\s*\d{1,2}:\d{2}:\d{2}[,.]\d{1,3}\s*-->\s*\d{1,2}:\d{2}:\d{2}[,.]\d{1,3}", re.MULTILINE
)


class SubtitleError(Exception):
    """The subtitle cannot be used (message in Portuguese, shown on the job page)."""


def decode_srt(data: bytes) -> str:
    """Brazilian subtitles come in UTF-8, Windows-1252 or UTF-16: never fail, keep accents."""
    if data.startswith((codecs.BOM_UTF16_LE, codecs.BOM_UTF16_BE)):
        text = data.decode("utf-16")
    else:
        try:
            text = data.decode("utf-8-sig")
        except UnicodeDecodeError:
            text = data.decode("cp1252", errors="replace")
    return text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n")


def count_cues(text: str) -> int:
    return len(TIMING.findall(text))


def prepare_srt(data: bytes, name: str) -> str:
    text = decode_srt(data)
    if count_cues(text) == 0:
        # spumux would succeed with no subtitle at all: refuse before it runs.
        raise SubtitleError(f"A legenda {name} está vazia ou inválida.")
    return text


def extract_command(source: Path, stream_index: int, output: Path) -> list[str]:
    return [
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-nostdin", "-i", str(source.absolute()),
        "-map", f"0:{stream_index}", "-c:s", "srt", str(output),
    ]  # fmt: skip


def spumux_xml(srt: Path, aspect: str) -> str:
    """DejaVu Sans 26 with an outline, inside a ~10% safe area.

    movie-height 478, not 480: a known spumux workaround (480 can push the last line out of
    the subpicture area). Both fps are 29.97 because SRT times are absolute; a different
    subtitle-fps would stretch them.
    """
    root = ET.Element("subpictures", format="NTSC")
    ET.SubElement(
        ET.SubElement(root, "stream"),
        "textsub",
        {
            "filename": str(srt), "characterset": "UTF-8", "font": str(FONT),
            "fontsize": "26.0", "fill-color": "white", "outline-color": "black",
            "outline-thickness": "2.5", "horizontal-alignment": "center",
            "vertical-alignment": "bottom", "left-margin": "72", "right-margin": "72",
            "top-margin": "48", "bottom-margin": "48", "movie-fps": "29.97",
            "subtitle-fps": "29.97", "movie-width": "720", "movie-height": "478",
            "aspect": aspect,
        },
    )  # fmt: skip
    return ET.tostring(root, encoding="unicode")


def spumux_command(xml: Path, stream_number: int) -> list[str]:
    """Reads the program stream on stdin and writes it, with one more stream, on stdout."""
    return ["spumux", "-s", str(stream_number), str(xml)]
```

  In `pipeline.py`, after the mux and the deletion of `video.m2v`/audio files, add the step:

```python
    progress = begin(Step.SUBTITLES)
    for number, subtitle in enumerate(plan.subtitles):
        srt = job_dir / f"sub{number}.srt"
        with _tool_step(Step.SUBTITLES):
            if subtitle.stream_index is not None:
                tools.run(extract_command(source, subtitle.stream_index, srt))
                data, name = srt.read_bytes(), f"interna {subtitle.stream_index}"
            else:
                assert subtitle.file is not None
                data, name = (media_dir / subtitle.file).read_bytes(), Path(subtitle.file).name
        try:
            srt.write_text(prepare_srt(data, name), encoding="utf-8")
        except SubtitleError as error:
            raise PipelineError(str(error), Step.SUBTITLES) from error
        xml_file = job_dir / f"sub{number}.xml"
        xml_file.write_text(spumux_xml(srt, plan.aspect), encoding="utf-8")
        rendered = job_dir / "movie.next.mpg"
        with _tool_step(Step.SUBTITLES):
            tools.run(spumux_command(xml_file, number), stdin=movie, stdout=rendered)
        os.replace(rendered, movie)
        srt.unlink()
        xml_file.unlink()
        progress((number + 1) / len(plan.subtitles))
```

```python
def srt_cp1252(path: Path) -> Path:
    text = ("1\r\n00:00:01,000 --> 00:00:03,000\r\nAção, coração e pão de açúcar!\r\n"
            "É você? Não, é ele.\r\n\r\n"
            "2\r\n00:00:05,000 --> 00:00:07,000\r\nUma fala com quarenta e dois caracteres\r\n"
            "e mais uma linha também bem comprida aqui\r\n")  # fmt: skip
    data = text.encode("cp1252")
    try:
        data.decode("utf-8")
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        path.write_bytes(data)
        return path
    raise AssertionError("the test subtitle must not be valid UTF-8")
```

## Group 2c: automatic QA

### Task 11: QA of the disc before it is published

**Files:**
- Create: `src/projeto_dvd/authoring/dvd/qa.py`
- Modify: `src/projeto_dvd/jobs/pipeline.py` (collect checks; QA step before the copy; `QaFailed`)
- Test: `tests/unit/test_qa.py`, `tests/unit/test_pipeline.py`, `tests/integration/test_dvd.py`, `tests/integration/clips.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StreamReport`, `inspect_stream` (Task 6); `PROFILES` (Task 2); `dvd_language` (Task 3); `DvdPlan` (Task 4); `Tools`, `RunResult` (Task 8); `run_pipeline` (Task 9).
- Produces:
  - `Check` (Pydantic, frozen): `name: str`, `label: str`, `ok: bool`, `detail: str = ""`, `warning: bool = False`.
  - `QaReport` (Pydantic, frozen): `checks: tuple[Check, ...]`, `iso_bytes: int`, `estimated_bytes: int`; property `passed: bool`; `first_failure: Check | None`.
  - `size_checks(iso_bytes: int, plan: DvdPlan) -> list[Check]`
  - `vob_check(dvd_dir: Path) -> Check`
  - `isoinfo_command(iso: Path) -> list[str]`; `tree_check(listing: str) -> Check`
  - `dvd_probe_command(iso: Path) -> list[str]`; `stream_checks(probe: dict[str, Any], plan: DvdPlan, video_seconds: float) -> list[Check]`
  - `extract_video_command(iso: Path, output: Path) -> list[str]`; `bitstream_checks(report: StreamReport, plan: DvdPlan) -> list[Check]`
  - `sync_probe_command(mpg: Path) -> list[str]`; `sync_check(probe: dict[str, Any], plan: DvdPlan) -> Check`
  - `encode_check(pass2: RunResult, mux: RunResult, author: RunResult) -> Check`
  - `decode_command(iso: Path) -> list[str]`; `decode_check(stderr: str) -> Check`
  - `pipeline.QaFailed(PipelineError)` with `report: QaReport`; `PipelineResult.qa_json` filled.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (`tests/unit/test_qa.py`)

```python
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest

from projeto_dvd.authoring.dvd import qa
from projeto_dvd.encode.mpeg2 import StreamReport
from projeto_dvd.jobs.runner import RunResult
from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import SubtitleChoice
from projeto_dvd.planner.profiles import DVD9
from tests.plans import make_plan
from tests.tree import make_file

PLAN = make_plan()


def test_size_fails_above_the_media_and_warns_above_the_margin() -> None:
    fits, margin = qa.size_checks(int(DVD9.capacity_bytes * 0.5), PLAN)
    assert fits.ok and margin.ok and not margin.warning

    fits, margin = qa.size_checks(int(DVD9.capacity_bytes * 0.98), PLAN)
    assert fits.ok and margin.ok and margin.warning

    fits, _ = qa.size_checks(DVD9.capacity_bytes + 1, PLAN)
    assert not fits.ok


def test_vob_check(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    make_file(tmp_path / "VIDEO_TS" / "VTS_01_1.VOB", b"x")
    assert qa.vob_check(tmp_path).ok

    big = tmp_path / "VIDEO_TS" / "VTS_01_2.VOB"
    with big.open("wb") as file:
        file.truncate(1024**3 + 1)
    assert not qa.vob_check(tmp_path).ok


LISTING = "\n".join([
    "/AUDIO_TS", "/VIDEO_TS", "/VIDEO_TS/VIDEO_TS.BUP;1", "/VIDEO_TS/VIDEO_TS.IFO;1",
    "/VIDEO_TS/VTS_01_0.BUP;1", "/VIDEO_TS/VTS_01_0.IFO;1", "/VIDEO_TS/VTS_01_1.VOB;1",
])  # fmt: skip


def test_tree_check() -> None:
    assert qa.tree_check(LISTING).ok
    broken = qa.tree_check(LISTING.replace("/VIDEO_TS/VTS_01_0.IFO;1", ""))
    assert not broken.ok and "VTS_01_0.IFO" in broken.detail


def probe(**changes: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    data: dict[str, Any] = {
        "streams": [
            {"codec_type": "video", "codec_name": "mpeg2video", "width": 720, "height": 480,
             "display_aspect_ratio": "16:9", "r_frame_rate": "30000/1001",
             "nb_read_packets": "300"},
            {"codec_type": "audio", "codec_name": "ac3", "tags": {"language": "por"},
             "nb_read_packets": "100"},
            {"codec_type": "audio", "codec_name": "ac3", "tags": {"language": "eng"},
             "nb_read_packets": "100"},
        ],
        "chapters": [{"start_time": "0.000000"}, {"start_time": "300.300000"}],
        "format": {"duration": "7200.4"},
    }  # fmt: skip
    data.update(changes)
    return data


def failing(checks: list[qa.Check]) -> list[str]:
    return [check.name for check in checks if not check.ok]


def test_streams_match_the_plan() -> None:
    assert failing(qa.stream_checks(probe(), PLAN, 7200.0)) == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("mutate", "name"),
    [
        (lambda d: d["streams"][0].update(width=704), "video"),
        (lambda d: d["streams"][0].update(display_aspect_ratio="4:3"), "video"),
        (lambda d: d["streams"][1]["tags"].update(language="spa"), "audio"),
        (lambda d: d["streams"][2].update(nb_read_packets="0"), "audio"),
        (lambda d: d["streams"].pop(), "audio"),
        (lambda d: d["chapters"].pop(), "chapters"),
        (lambda d: d["format"].update(duration="7100"), "duration"),
    ],
)
def test_stream_failures(mutate: Any, name: str) -> None:
    data = probe()
    mutate(data)

    assert name in failing(qa.stream_checks(data, PLAN, 7200.0))


def test_subtitles_must_have_packets() -> None:
    plan = make_plan(subtitles=(SubtitleChoice(key="s3", stream_index=3, file=None,
                                               language="pt"),))  # fmt: skip
    data = probe()
    data["streams"].append({"codec_type": "subtitle", "codec_name": "dvd_subtitle",
                            "tags": {"language": "por"}, "nb_read_packets": "0"})  # fmt: skip

    assert "subtitles" in failing(qa.stream_checks(data, plan, 7200.0))


def report(**fields: Any) -> StreamReport:
    base = StreamReport(frame_rate_codes={4}, progressive_sequence={0}, aspect_codes={3},
                        pictures=100, repeated=50, max_gop=12)  # fmt: skip
    for key, value in fields.items():
        setattr(base, key, value)
    return base


def test_bitstream_checks() -> None:
    assert failing(qa.bitstream_checks(report(), PLAN)) == []
    assert failing(qa.bitstream_checks(report(frame_rate_codes={1}), PLAN)) == ["bitstream"]
    assert failing(qa.bitstream_checks(report(progressive_sequence={1}), PLAN)) == ["bitstream"]
    assert failing(qa.bitstream_checks(report(open_gops=1), PLAN)) == ["gop"]
    assert failing(qa.bitstream_checks(report(max_gop=13), PLAN)) == ["gop"]
    assert failing(qa.bitstream_checks(report(parity_breaks=2), PLAN)) == ["fields"]
    assert failing(qa.bitstream_checks(report(aspect_codes={2}), PLAN)) == ["bitstream"]


def packets(*items: tuple[int, float]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "streams": [{"index": 0, "codec_type": "video", "id": "0x1e0"},
                    {"index": 1, "codec_type": "audio", "id": "0x80"},
                    {"index": 2, "codec_type": "audio", "id": "0x81"}],
        "packets": [{"stream_index": i, "pts_time": f"{t:.6f}"} for i, t in items],
    }  # fmt: skip


def test_sync_check() -> None:
    good = packets((1, 0.5556), (0, 0.5556), (2, 0.6056), (0, 0.589))
    assert qa.sync_check(good, PLAN).ok  # second audio keeps its 50 ms delay

    late = packets((0, 0.5556), (1, 0.5000), (2, 0.6056))
    assert not qa.sync_check(late, PLAN).ok


def test_encode_check() -> None:
    clean = RunResult({"dup_frames": "0", "drop_frames": "0"}, "")
    assert qa.encode_check(clean, RunResult({}, ""), RunResult({}, "")).ok
    duplicated = RunResult({"dup_frames": "60", "drop_frames": "0"}, "")
    assert not qa.encode_check(duplicated, RunResult({}, ""), RunResult({}, "")).ok
    underflow = RunResult({}, "[dvd @ 0x1] buffer underflow st=0")
    assert not qa.encode_check(clean, underflow, RunResult({}, "")).ok
    author = RunResult({}, "ERR:  no video format specified")
    assert not qa.encode_check(clean, RunResult({}, ""), author).ok


def test_decode_check() -> None:
    assert qa.decode_check("").ok
    assert not qa.decode_check("[mpeg2video @ 0x1] ac-tex damaged at 10 20\n").ok


def test_commands() -> None:
    iso = Path("/w/disc.iso")
    assert qa.isoinfo_command(iso) == ["isoinfo", "-f", "-i", "/w/disc.iso"]
    assert qa.dvd_probe_command(iso)[:9] == [
        "ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-f", "dvdvideo", "-title", "1", "-count_packets",
        "-show_streams",
    ]  # fmt: skip
    extract = qa.extract_video_command(iso, Path("/w/qa.m2v"))
    assert extract[extract.index("-map") + 1] == "0:v" and extract[-1] == "/w/qa.m2v"
    decode = qa.decode_command(iso)
    assert decode[decode.index("-v") + 1] == "error"
    assert decode.count("-map") == 2 and decode[-3:] == ["-f", "null", "-"]


def test_report_round_trip_and_first_failure() -> None:
    checks = (qa.Check(name="a", label="A", ok=True),
              qa.Check(name="b", label="B", ok=False, detail="x"))  # fmt: skip
    result = qa.QaReport(checks=checks, iso_bytes=1, estimated_bytes=2)

    assert not result.passed
    assert result.first_failure == checks[1]
    assert qa.QaReport.model_validate_json(result.model_dump_json()) == result
```

  and `inspect_stream` is monkeypatched to return a good `StreamReport`. New tests:

  - `test_failed_qa_keeps_the_iso_in_work_and_publishes_nothing` (bitstream report with

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `src/projeto_dvd/authoring/dvd/qa.py`

```python
"""Automatic QA of a DVD ISO (items 1 to 3 of the checklist, and the bitstream).

Commands are built here and run by the pipeline; the checks are pure functions over their
output. A failed check keeps the ISO out of the output folder.
"""

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict

from projeto_dvd.api.formatting import format_size
from projeto_dvd.encode.mpeg2 import NTSC_RATE_CODE, StreamReport
from projeto_dvd.jobs.runner import RunResult
from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import DvdPlan
from projeto_dvd.planner.languages import dvd_language
from projeto_dvd.planner.profiles import PROFILES

GIB = 1024**3
REQUIRED_TREE = (
    "/AUDIO_TS", "/VIDEO_TS/VIDEO_TS.IFO", "/VIDEO_TS/VIDEO_TS.BUP",
    "/VIDEO_TS/VTS_01_0.IFO", "/VIDEO_TS/VTS_01_0.BUP", "/VIDEO_TS/VTS_01_1.VOB",
)  # fmt: skip
ASPECT_CODES = {"16:9": 3, "4:3": 2}
MAX_GOP = 12
CHAPTER_TOLERANCE = 1.0
DURATION_TOLERANCE = 1.0
SYNC_TOLERANCE = 0.005


class Check(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(frozen=True)

    name: str
    label: str
    ok: bool
    detail: str = ""
    warning: bool = False


class QaReport(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(frozen=True)

    checks: tuple[Check, ...]
    iso_bytes: int
    estimated_bytes: int

    @property
    def passed(self) -> bool:
        return all(check.ok for check in self.checks)

    @property
    def first_failure(self) -> Check | None:
        return next((check for check in self.checks if not check.ok), None)


def size_checks(iso_bytes: int, plan: DvdPlan) -> list[Check]:
    profile = PROFILES[plan.profile]
    size = f"{format_size(iso_bytes)} de {format_size(profile.capacity_bytes)}"
    margin = int(profile.margin * profile.capacity_bytes)
    return [
        Check(name="size", label=f"Cabe num {profile.name}", ok=iso_bytes <= profile.capacity_bytes,
              detail=size),
        Check(name="margin", label="Dentro da margem de 97%", ok=True,
              warning=iso_bytes > margin,
              detail="Passou da margem de segurança, mas cabe na mídia." if iso_bytes > margin
              else ""),
    ]  # fmt: skip


def vob_check(dvd_dir: Path) -> Check:
    big = [vob.name for vob in sorted((dvd_dir / "VIDEO_TS").glob("*.VOB"))
           if vob.stat().st_size > GIB]  # fmt: skip
    return Check(name="vob", label="VOBs de até 1 GB", ok=not big, detail=", ".join(big))


def isoinfo_command(iso: Path) -> list[str]:
    return ["isoinfo", "-f", "-i", str(iso)]


def tree_check(listing: str) -> Check:
    found = {line.strip().split(";")[0].upper() for line in listing.splitlines() if line.strip()}
    missing = [path for path in REQUIRED_TREE if path not in found]
    return Check(name="tree", label="Estrutura VIDEO_TS completa", ok=not missing,
                 detail="Faltando: " + ", ".join(missing) if missing else "")  # fmt: skip


def dvd_probe_command(iso: Path) -> list[str]:
    return [
        "ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-f", "dvdvideo", "-title", "1", "-count_packets",
        "-show_streams", "-show_chapters", "-show_format", "-of", "json", str(iso),
    ]  # fmt: skip


def stream_checks(probe: dict[str, Any], plan: DvdPlan, video_seconds: float) -> list[Check]:
    streams = probe.get("streams", [])
    by_type = {
        kind: [s for s in streams if s.get("codec_type") == kind]
        for kind in ("video", "audio", "subtitle")
    }
    video = by_type["video"][0] if by_type["video"] else {}
    video_ok = (
        video.get("codec_name") == "mpeg2video"
        and (video.get("width"), video.get("height")) == (720, 480)
        and video.get("display_aspect_ratio") == plan.aspect
        and video.get("r_frame_rate") == "30000/1001"
    )
    starts = [float(c.get("start_time", 0)) for c in probe.get("chapters", [])]
    chapters_ok = len(starts) == len(plan.chapters) and all(
        abs(a - b) <= CHAPTER_TOLERANCE for a, b in zip(starts, plan.chapters, strict=True)
    )
    duration = float(probe.get("format", {}).get("duration", 0))
    return [
        Check(name="video", label="Vídeo MPEG-2 720x480 NTSC", ok=video_ok,
              detail=_describe(video)),
        _tracks_check("audio", "Faixas de áudio", by_type["audio"], "ac3",
                      [a.language for a in plan.audio]),
        _tracks_check("subtitles", "Legendas", by_type["subtitle"], "dvd_subtitle",
                      [s.language for s in plan.subtitles]),
        Check(name="chapters", label="Capítulos", ok=chapters_ok,
              detail=f"{len(starts)} no disco, {len(plan.chapters)} planejados"),
        Check(name="duration", label="Duração", ok=abs(duration - video_seconds)
              <= DURATION_TOLERANCE, detail=f"{duration:.1f} s no disco, "
              f"{video_seconds:.1f} s codificados"),
    ]  # fmt: skip


def extract_video_command(iso: Path, output: Path) -> list[str]:
    return [
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-nostdin", "-v", "error", "-f", "dvdvideo", "-title", "1",
        "-i", str(iso), "-map", "0:v", "-c", "copy", "-f", "mpeg2video", str(output),
    ]  # fmt: skip


def bitstream_checks(report: StreamReport, plan: DvdPlan) -> list[Check]:
    flags_ok = (
        report.frame_rate_codes == {NTSC_RATE_CODE}
        and report.progressive_sequence == {0}
        and report.aspect_codes == {ASPECT_CODES[plan.aspect]}
    )
    return [
        Check(name="bitstream", label="Vídeo marcado como 29,97 entrelaçado (padrão DVD)",
              ok=flags_ok, detail=f"frame_rate_code {sorted(report.frame_rate_codes)}, "
              f"progressive_sequence {sorted(report.progressive_sequence)}"),
        Check(name="gop", label="GOPs fechados de até 12 quadros",
              ok=report.open_gops == 0 and report.max_gop <= MAX_GOP,
              detail=f"maior GOP {report.max_gop}, abertos {report.open_gops}"),
        Check(name="fields", label="Ordem dos campos contínua (sem tremida)",
              ok=report.parity_breaks == 0, detail=f"{report.parity_breaks} quebras"),
    ]  # fmt: skip


def sync_probe_command(mpg: Path) -> list[str]:
    return [
        "ffprobe", "-v", "error", "-read_intervals", "%+#2000",
        "-show_entries", "stream=index,codec_type,id:packet=stream_index,pts_time",
        "-of", "json", str(mpg),
    ]  # fmt: skip


def sync_check(probe: dict[str, Any], plan: DvdPlan) -> Check:
    """Each audio starts at the first video frame plus its own delay in the source."""
    streams = probe.get("streams", [])
    first: dict[int, float] = {}
    for packet in probe.get("packets", []):
        if packet.get("pts_time") not in (None, "N/A"):
            index = int(packet["stream_index"])
            first[index] = min(first.get(index, float("inf")), float(packet["pts_time"]))
    video = next((s["index"] for s in streams if s.get("codec_type") == "video"), None)
    audios = sorted((s for s in streams if s.get("codec_type") == "audio"),
                    key=lambda s: int(str(s.get("id", "0")), 0))  # fmt: skip
    if video not in first or len(audios) != len(plan.audio):
        return Check(name="sync", label="Áudio sincronizado", ok=False, detail="sem pacotes")
    gaps = [first.get(s["index"], float("inf")) - first[video] - max(a.offset, 0.0)
            for s, a in zip(audios, plan.audio, strict=True)]  # fmt: skip
    worst = max(abs(gap) for gap in gaps)
    return Check(name="sync", label="Áudio sincronizado", ok=worst <= SYNC_TOLERANCE,
                 detail=f"diferença máxima {worst * 1000:.1f} ms")  # fmt: skip


def encode_check(pass2: RunResult, mux: RunResult, author: RunResult) -> Check:
    duplicated = pass2.progress.get("dup_frames", "0") != "0"
    dropped = pass2.progress.get("drop_frames", "0") != "0"
    problems = [
        text
        for text, bad in (
            ("quadros duplicados", duplicated),
            ("quadros descartados", dropped),
            ("buffer underflow no mux", "buffer underflow" in mux.stderr),
            ("erro do dvdauthor", "ERR:" in author.stderr),
        )
        if bad
    ]
    return Check(name="encode", label="Codificação sem quadros duplicados", ok=not problems,
                 detail=", ".join(problems))  # fmt: skip


def decode_command(iso: Path) -> list[str]:
    return [
        "ffmpeg", "-nostdin", "-v", "error", "-f", "dvdvideo", "-title", "1", "-i", str(iso),
        "-map", "0:v", "-map", "0:a", "-progress", "pipe:1", "-nostats", "-f", "null", "-",
    ]  # fmt: skip


def decode_check(stderr: str) -> Check:
    errors = [line for line in stderr.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    return Check(name="decode", label="Disco inteiro decodificado sem erros", ok=not errors,
                 detail=errors[0][:200] if errors else "")  # fmt: skip


def _tracks_check(
    name: str, label: str, streams: list[dict[str, Any]], codec: str,
    languages: list[str | None],
) -> Check:  # fmt: skip
    ok = len(streams) == len(languages) and all(
        s.get("codec_name") == codec
        and int(s.get("nb_read_packets", 0)) > 0
        and (language is None or dvd_language(s.get("tags", {}).get("language")) == language)
        for s, language in zip(streams, languages, strict=False)
    )
    found = [
        f"{dvd_language(s.get('tags', {}).get('language')) or '?'} "
        f"({s.get('nb_read_packets', 0)} pacotes)"
        for s in streams
    ]
    return Check(name=name, label=label, ok=ok, detail=", ".join(found))


def _describe(video: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return (
        f"{video.get('codec_name')} {video.get('width')}x{video.get('height')} "
        f"{video.get('display_aspect_ratio')} {video.get('r_frame_rate')}"
    )
```

  In `pipeline.py`:
  - `class QaFailed(PipelineError)` with `report: QaReport`; message: `"A conferência
    automática reprovou: {label}. O ISO ficou na pasta de trabalho para inspeção."`
  - keep the `RunResult` of pass 2, the mux and dvdauthor;
  - after the mux (before deleting `movie.mpg`'s inputs), `sync = sync_check(json.loads(
    tools.capture(sync_probe_command(movie))), plan)`;
  - after dvdauthor, `vob = vob_check(job_dir / "dvd")`;
  - QA step after `genisoimage`:

```python
    progress = begin(Step.QA)
    with _tool_step(Step.QA):
        checks = [*size_checks(iso.stat().st_size, plan), vob,
                  tree_check(tools.capture(isoinfo_command(iso)))]  # fmt: skip
        checks += stream_checks(json.loads(tools.capture(dvd_probe_command(iso))), plan,
                                video_seconds)  # fmt: skip
        extracted = job_dir / "qa.m2v"
        tools.run(extract_video_command(iso, extracted))
        try:
            checks += bitstream_checks(inspect_stream(extracted), plan)
        finally:
            extracted.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        checks += [sync, encode_check(pass2, mux, author)]
        decode = tools.run(decode_command(iso), duration=plan.duration, on_progress=progress)
        checks.append(decode_check(decode.stderr))
    report = QaReport(checks=tuple(checks), iso_bytes=iso.stat().st_size,
                      estimated_bytes=plan.estimated_bytes)  # fmt: skip
    if not report.passed:
        raise QaFailed(report)
```

    where `video_seconds = int(pass2.progress.get("out_time_us", "0") or 0) / 1e6` (the length
    of the encoded video, so a longer audio in the source does not fail the check; fall back
    to `plan.duration` when the value is missing or `N/A`). `decode_check` must only see the
    tool's own stderr: `RunResult.stderr` starts after the `$ command` line already.
  - `PipelineResult(iso_path=..., qa_json=report.model_dump_json())`.

`clips.py`:

```python
def dvd_rip_film(path: Path, seconds: int = 10) -> Path:
    """A MakeMKV-like rip of a film DVD: MPEG-2 24p with soft pulldown, remuxed to MKV."""
    from projeto_dvd.encode.mpeg2 import apply_soft_pulldown

    work = path.parent
    work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    m2v, vob = work / "rip.m2v", work / "rip.vob"
    ffmpeg("-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"testsrc2=s=720x480:r=24000/1001:d={seconds}",
           "-c:v", "mpeg2video", "-b:v", "6000k", "-g", "12", "-bf", "2", "-flags", "+cgop",
           "-sc_threshold", "1000000000", "-aspect", "16:9", "-f", "mpeg2video", str(m2v))  # fmt: skip
    apply_soft_pulldown(m2v)
    ffmpeg("-fflags", "+genpts", "-i", str(m2v), "-f", "lavfi", "-i",
           f"sine=d={seconds}:sample_rate=48000", "-map", "0", "-map", "1", "-c:v", "copy",
           "-c:a", "ac3", "-b:a", "192k", "-metadata:s:a:0", "language=por",
           "-f", "dvd", str(vob))  # fmt: skip
    ffmpeg("-fflags", "+genpts", "-i", str(vob), "-map", "0", "-c", "copy", str(path))
    m2v.unlink()
    vob.unlink()
    return path


def interlaced_480i(path: Path, seconds: int = 8) -> Path:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    ffmpeg("-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"testsrc2=s=720x480:r=30000/1001:d={seconds}",
           "-f", "lavfi", "-i", f"sine=d={seconds}:sample_rate=48000",
           "-vf", "setfield=tff", "-c:v", "mpeg2video", "-flags", "+ilme+ildct", "-top", "1",
           "-b:v", "6000k", "-aspect", "4:3", "-c:a", "ac3", "-b:a", "192k",
           "-metadata:s:a:0", "language=por", str(path))  # fmt: skip
    return path
```

```python
from projeto_dvd.authoring.dvd.qa import QaReport


def passed(result_qa: str | None) -> QaReport:
    assert result_qa is not None
    report = QaReport.model_validate_json(result_qa)
    assert report.passed, [c for c in report.checks if not c.ok]
    return report


def test_1080p_movie_passes_the_qa(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    media = tmp_path / "media"
    clips.movie_1080p(media / "Filme" / "Filme.mkv")
    clips.srt_cp1252(media / "Filme" / "Filme.pt-BR.srt")

    result = generate(media, "Filme", tmp_path)

    names = {check.name for check in passed(result.qa_json).checks}
    assert {"size", "tree", "video", "audio", "subtitles", "chapters", "bitstream", "fields",
            "sync", "decode"} <= names  # fmt: skip
    crop = subprocess.run(
        ["ffmpeg", "-nostdin", "-f", "dvdvideo", "-title", "1", "-i", str(result.iso_path),
         "-vf", "cropdetect=limit=24:round=2", "-frames:v", "60", "-f", "null", "-"],
        capture_output=True, text=True, check=True,
    ).stderr  # fmt: skip
    height = int(crop.rsplit("crop=", 1)[1].split(":")[1])
    assert abs(height - 356) <= 4


def test_dvd_rip_film_is_not_duplicated(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

    result = generate(media, "Rip", tmp_path)

    passed(result.qa_json)  # includes dup_frames == 0 and the 3:2 field cadence


def test_480i_source_stays_interlaced(tmp_path: Path) -> None:

    clips.interlaced_480i(media / "Serie" / "Ep.mkv")
    source = clips.disc_source(media, "Serie")
    assert source.summary.video is not None and source.summary.video.field_order == "tb"
    assert (source.mode, source.top_field_first) == (VideoMode.INTERLACED, True)

    result = generate(media, "Serie", tmp_path)

    report = passed(result.qa_json)
    assert any(c.name == "video" and "4:3" in c.detail for c in report.checks)
```
