```python
def test_qa_rejects_a_disc_without_pulldown(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    media = tmp_path / "media"
    clips.movie_1080p(media / "Filme" / "Filme.mkv")
    monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline_module, "apply_soft_pulldown", lambda path, checkpoint: 0)

    with pytest.raises(QaFailed) as caught:
        generate(media, "Filme", tmp_path, subtitles=(), subtitle_on=None)

    failure = caught.value.report.first_failure
    assert failure is not None and failure.name == "bitstream"
    assert not (tmp_path / "out" / "iso" / "Filme.iso").exists()


def test_cancel_stops_ffmpeg(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    media = tmp_path / "media"
    clips.movie_1080p(media / "Filme" / "Filme.mkv", seconds=60)
    plan = clips.plan_for(media, "Filme")
    job = tmp_path / "work" / "jobs" / "1"
    job.mkdir(parents=True)
    state: dict[str, float] = {}

    def report(step: Step, fraction: float) -> None:
        if step == Step.VIDEO_PASS1 and fraction > 0:
            state["started"] = fraction

    runner = ProcessRunner(log_path=job / "job.log", should_cancel=lambda: "started" in state,
                           cwd=job, poll_seconds=0.1)  # fmt: skip
    with pytest.raises(Canceled):
        run_pipeline(plan, media_dir=media, job_dir=job, output_dir=tmp_path / "out",
                     tools=runner, report=report, should_cancel=lambda: "started" in state)  # fmt: skip

    alive = [p for p in Path("/proc").iterdir()
             if p.name.isdecimal() and b"ffmpeg" in (p / "cmdline").read_bytes()]  # fmt: skip
    assert alive == []
```

## Group 3: jobs and the worker

### Task 12: Job table, WAL and atomic transitions

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/projeto_dvd/db/models.py`, `src/projeto_dvd/db/session.py`
- Create: `src/projeto_dvd/db/migrations/versions/0002_create_job.py`, `src/projeto_dvd/jobs/store.py`
- Test: `tests/unit/test_db.py`, `tests/unit/test_store.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DvdPlan` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `Job` SQLModel table (`job`): `id, folder (index), plan_json, status, cancel_requested, step, progress, error, qa_json, iso_path, created_at, started_at, finished_at`; partial unique index `ix_job_active_folder`.
  - `session.make_engine(url)` now sets `PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL` and `timeout=5`; `session.is_migrated(engine: Engine) -> bool`.
  - `store.JobStatus(StrEnum)`: `QUEUED="queued"`, `RUNNING="running"`, `DONE="done"`, `FAILED="failed"`, `CANCELED="canceled"`; `ACTIVE = (QUEUED, RUNNING)`.
  - `store.ActiveJobError(Exception)` with `job_id: int`.
  - `store.as_utc(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None`
  - `store.enqueue(session: Session, plan: DvdPlan) -> int`
  - `store.get_job(session, job_id: int) -> Job | None`, `active_job(session, folder) -> Job | None`, `latest_job(session, folder) -> Job | None`, `active_folders(session) -> dict[str, Job]`, `running_job(session) -> Job | None`, `list_jobs(session) -> list[Job]`
  - `store.claim_next(engine: Engine) -> int | None`
  - `store.request_cancel(engine, job_id: int) -> None`
  - `store.cancel_requested(engine, job_id: int) -> bool`
  - `store.save_progress(engine, job_id: int, step: str, progress: float) -> None`

```python
from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import Engine
from sqlmodel import Session

from projeto_dvd.jobs import store
from projeto_dvd.jobs.store import ActiveJobError, JobStatus
from tests.plans import make_plan


def enqueue(session: Session, folder: str = "Filme") -> int:
    return store.enqueue(session, make_plan(folder=folder))


def status(engine: Engine, job_id: int) -> str:
    with Session(engine) as session:
        job = store.get_job(session, job_id)
        assert job is not None
        return job.status


def test_enqueue_stores_the_plan(session: Session) -> None:
    job_id = enqueue(session)

    job = store.get_job(session, job_id)
    assert job is not None
    assert (job.folder, job.status, job.progress) == ("Filme", "queued", 0.0)
    assert job.plan_json == make_plan().model_dump_json()


def test_one_active_job_per_folder(session: Session) -> None:
    first = enqueue(session)

    with pytest.raises(ActiveJobError) as caught:
        enqueue(session)

    assert caught.value.job_id == first
    assert enqueue(session, "Outro") != first


def test_a_finished_job_frees_the_folder(engine: Engine, session: Session) -> None:
    first = enqueue(session)
    store.claim_next(engine)
    store.finish(engine, first, JobStatus.DONE, iso_path="/output/iso/Filme.iso")

    assert enqueue(session) != first


def test_claim_takes_the_oldest_once(engine: Engine, session: Session) -> None:
    first, second = enqueue(session, "A"), enqueue(session, "B")

    assert store.claim_next(engine) == first
    assert store.claim_next(engine) == second
    assert store.claim_next(engine) is None
    assert status(engine, first) == "running"


def test_cancel_a_queued_job_at_once(engine: Engine, session: Session) -> None:
    job_id = enqueue(session)

    store.request_cancel(engine, job_id)

    assert status(engine, job_id) == "canceled"
    assert store.claim_next(engine) is None


def test_cancel_after_the_claim_only_asks(engine: Engine, session: Session) -> None:
    job_id = enqueue(session)
    store.claim_next(engine)

    store.request_cancel(engine, job_id)

    assert status(engine, job_id) == "running"
    assert store.cancel_requested(engine, job_id)


def test_finish_only_moves_running_jobs(engine: Engine, session: Session) -> None:
    job_id = enqueue(session)
    store.request_cancel(engine, job_id)

    assert not store.finish(engine, job_id, JobStatus.DONE)
    assert status(engine, job_id) == "canceled"


def test_progress_is_saved_while_running(engine: Engine, session: Session) -> None:
    job_id = enqueue(session)
    store.claim_next(engine)

    store.save_progress(engine, job_id, "video_pass2", 0.42)

    session.expire_all()
    job = store.get_job(session, job_id)
    assert job is not None and (job.step, job.progress) == ("video_pass2", 0.42)


def test_fail_interrupted(engine: Engine, session: Session) -> None:
    running, queued = enqueue(session, "A"), enqueue(session, "B")
    store.claim_next(engine)

    assert store.fail_interrupted(engine, "reiniciou") == 1

    session.expire_all()
    job = store.get_job(session, running)
    assert job is not None and (job.status, job.error) == ("failed", "reiniciou")
    assert status(engine, queued) == "queued"


def test_queries(engine: Engine, session: Session) -> None:
    a, b = enqueue(session, "A"), enqueue(session, "B")
    store.claim_next(engine)

    assert set(store.active_folders(session)) == {"A", "B"}
    running = store.running_job(session)
    assert running is not None and running.id == a
    latest = store.latest_job(session, "B")
    assert latest is not None and latest.id == b
    assert [job.id for job in store.list_jobs(session)] == [b, a]


def test_dates_come_back_in_utc(session: Session) -> None:
    job = store.get_job(session, enqueue(session))
    assert job is not None

    created = store.as_utc(job.created_at)

    assert created is not None and created.tzinfo == UTC
    assert datetime.now(UTC) - created < timedelta(minutes=1)
    assert store.as_utc(None) is None
```

`models.py` (add; imports `Index`, `text` from `sqlalchemy`):

```python
class Job(SQLModel, table=True):
    """One DVD generation. The plan is stored whole; the log lives in WORK_DIR/jobs/<id>/."""

    __tablename__ = "job"
    # At most one queued or running job per disc folder: two quick clicks cannot create two.
    __table_args__ = (
        Index(
            "ix_job_active_folder",
            "folder",
            unique=True,
            sqlite_where=text("status IN ('queued', 'running')"),
        ),
    )

    id: int | None = Field(default=None, primary_key=True)
    folder: str = Field(index=True)
    plan_json: str
    status: str = "queued"
    cancel_requested: bool = False
    step: str | None = None
    progress: float = 0.0
    error: str | None = None  # Portuguese, for the job page
    qa_json: str | None = None
    iso_path: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime
    started_at: datetime | None = None
    finished_at: datetime | None = None
```

```python
import sqlalchemy as sa

revision = "0002"
down_revision = "0001"
branch_labels = None

        "job",

    )

        "job",

        unique=True,
        sqlite_where=sa.text("status IN ('queued', 'running')"),
    )


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.drop_table("job")
```

`session.py`:

```python
from typing import Any

from alembic.runtime.migration import MigrationContext
from alembic.script import ScriptDirectory
from sqlalchemy import Engine, event


def make_engine(url: str) -> Engine:
    # FastAPI runs sync routes in a thread pool, so connections cross threads. timeout is the
    # SQLite busy timeout: the web and the worker share the database.
    engine = create_engine(url, connect_args={"check_same_thread": False, "timeout": 5})

    @event.listens_for(engine, "connect")
    def _wal(connection: Any, _record: Any) -> None:
        # WAL: readers never wait for the worker's writes. Works on the ext4 "data" volume.
        cursor = connection.cursor()
        cursor.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL")
        cursor.close()

    return engine


def is_migrated(engine: Engine) -> bool:
    """True when the database is at the latest migration (the worker waits for the web)."""
    config = _config(str(engine.url))
    head = ScriptDirectory.from_config(config).get_current_head()
    with engine.connect() as connection:
        return MigrationContext.configure(connection).get_current_revision() == head
```

  (Extract the `Config` building of `migrate()` into `_config(url) -> Config` and use it in
  both. `str(engine.url)` keeps the path for SQLite URLs.)

`src/projeto_dvd/jobs/store.py`:

```python
"""The job queue in SQLite: atomic transitions shared by the web and the worker."""

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from enum import StrEnum

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Engine, bindparam, text
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlmodel import Session, col, select

from projeto_dvd.db.models import Job
from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import DvdPlan


class JobStatus(StrEnum):
    QUEUED = "queued"
    RUNNING = "running"
    DONE = "done"
    FAILED = "failed"
    CANCELED = "canceled"


ACTIVE = (JobStatus.QUEUED, JobStatus.RUNNING)


class ActiveJobError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, job_id: int) -> None:
        super().__init__(f"job {job_id} is already active for this folder")
        self.job_id = job_id


# One statement each, so a cancel and the worker's claim cannot overwrite each other.
CLAIM = text(
    """
    UPDATE job
    SET status = 'running'
      , started_at = :now
    WHERE id = (
        SELECT id
        FROM job
        WHERE status = 'queued'
        ORDER BY id
        LIMIT 1
    )
      AND status = 'queued'
    RETURNING id
    """
).bindparams(bindparam("now", type_=DateTime()))
CANCEL_QUEUED = text(
    """
    UPDATE job
    SET status = 'canceled'
      , finished_at = :now
    WHERE id = :id
      AND status = 'queued'
    """
).bindparams(bindparam("now", type_=DateTime()))
ASK_CANCEL = text(
    """
    UPDATE job
    SET cancel_requested = 1
    WHERE id = :id
      AND status = 'running'
    """
)
SAVE_PROGRESS = text(
    """
    UPDATE job
    SET step = :step
      , progress = :progress
    WHERE id = :id
      AND status = 'running'
    """
)
FINISH = text(
    """
    UPDATE job
    SET status = :status
      , error = :error
      , qa_json = :qa_json
      , iso_path = :iso_path
      , finished_at = :now
    WHERE id = :id
      AND status = 'running'
    """
).bindparams(bindparam("now", type_=DateTime()))
FAIL_INTERRUPTED = text(
    """
    UPDATE job
    SET status = 'failed'
      , error = :error
      , finished_at = :now
    WHERE status = 'running'
    """
).bindparams(bindparam("now", type_=DateTime()))


def as_utc(value: datetime | None) -> datetime | None:
    """SQLite gives naive datetimes back; they were written in UTC."""
    if value is None or value.tzinfo is not None:
        return value
    return value.replace(tzinfo=UTC)


def enqueue(session: Session, plan: DvdPlan) -> int:
    job = Job(folder=plan.folder, plan_json=plan.model_dump_json(), created_at=_now())
    session.add(job)
    try:
        session.commit()
    except IntegrityError as error:
        session.rollback()
        existing = active_job(session, plan.folder)
        if existing is None or existing.id is None:  # finished in between: try once more
            raise
        raise ActiveJobError(existing.id) from error
    assert job.id is not None
    return job.id


def get_job(session: Session, job_id: int) -> Job | None:
    return session.get(Job, job_id)


def active_job(session: Session, folder: str) -> Job | None:
    query = select(Job).where(Job.folder == folder, col(Job.status).in_(list(ACTIVE)))
    return session.exec(query).first()


def latest_job(session: Session, folder: str) -> Job | None:
    query = select(Job).where(Job.folder == folder).order_by(col(Job.id).desc())
    return session.exec(query).first()


def active_folders(session: Session) -> dict[str, Job]:
    query = select(Job).where(col(Job.status).in_(list(ACTIVE)))
    return {job.folder: job for job in session.exec(query).all()}


def running_job(session: Session) -> Job | None:
    return session.exec(select(Job).where(Job.status == JobStatus.RUNNING)).first()


def list_jobs(session: Session) -> list[Job]:
    return list(session.exec(select(Job).order_by(col(Job.id).desc())).all())


def claim_next(engine: Engine) -> int | None:
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        job_id: int | None = connection.execute(CLAIM, {"now": _now()}).scalar_one_or_none()
        return job_id


def request_cancel(engine: Engine, job_id: int) -> None:
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        canceled = connection.execute(CANCEL_QUEUED, {"id": job_id, "now": _now()}).rowcount
        if not canceled:
            connection.execute(ASK_CANCEL, {"id": job_id})


def cancel_requested(engine: Engine, job_id: int) -> bool:
    with engine.connect() as connection:
        value = connection.execute(
            text("SELECT cancel_requested FROM job WHERE id = :id"), {"id": job_id}
        ).scalar_one_or_none()
    return bool(value)


def save_progress(engine: Engine, job_id: int, step: str, progress: float) -> None:
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.execute(SAVE_PROGRESS, {"id": job_id, "step": step, "progress": progress})


def finish(
    engine: Engine,
    job_id: int,
    status: JobStatus,
    *,
    error: str | None = None,
    qa_json: str | None = None,
    iso_path: str | None = None,
) -> bool:
    values = {"id": job_id, "status": str(status), "error": error, "qa_json": qa_json,
              "iso_path": iso_path, "now": _now()}  # fmt: skip
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        return bool(connection.execute(FINISH, values).rowcount)


def fail_interrupted(engine: Engine, message: str) -> int:
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        return int(connection.execute(FAIL_INTERRUPTED, {"error": message, "now": _now()}).rowcount)


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(UTC)
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:
  - `RESTARTED`, `STOPPED`, `UNEXPECTED` texts.
  - `Worker(engine: Engine, settings: Settings, make_tools: Callable[[Path, Callable[[], bool]], Tools] = default_tools, clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic, stop: threading.Event = field(default_factory=threading.Event))` with `recover() -> None`, `run_once() -> bool`, `handle_signal(signum: int, frame: object) -> None`.
  - `default_tools(job_dir: Path, should_cancel: Callable[[], bool]) -> Tools`
  - `serve(worker: Worker, engine: Engine, poll_seconds: float = 2.0) -> None`

```python
import signal
from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import Engine
from sqlmodel import Session

from projeto_dvd.config import Settings
from projeto_dvd.jobs import store, worker
from projeto_dvd.jobs.pipeline import PipelineError, PipelineResult, QaFailed, Step
from projeto_dvd.jobs.runner import Canceled
from projeto_dvd.jobs.worker import Worker
from projeto_dvd.authoring.dvd.qa import Check, QaReport
from tests.plans import make_plan
from tests.tree import make_file


@pytest.fixture
def settings(tmp_path: Path, database_url: str) -> Settings:
    return Settings(media_dir=tmp_path / "media", work_dir=tmp_path / "work",
                    output_dir=tmp_path / "out", database_url=database_url)  # fmt: skip


def make_worker(engine: Engine, settings: Settings, clock: list[float] | None = None) -> Worker:
    ticks = clock if clock is not None else [0.0]
    return Worker(engine, settings, make_tools=lambda job_dir, cancel: object(),  # type: ignore[arg-type, return-value]
                  clock=lambda: ticks[0])  # fmt: skip


def job(engine: Engine, job_id: int) -> Any:
    with Session(engine) as session:
        return store.get_job(session, job_id)


def queue(session: Session, folder: str = "Filme") -> int:
    return store.enqueue(session, make_plan(folder=folder))


def fake_pipeline(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
                  behaviour: Callable[..., PipelineResult]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:  # fmt: skip
    calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def run(plan: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> PipelineResult:
        calls.append({"plan": plan, **kwargs})
        return behaviour(plan, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "run_pipeline", run)
    return calls


def test_idle_worker_returns_false(engine: Engine, settings: Settings) -> None:
    assert make_worker(engine, settings).run_once() is False


def test_done_job_keeps_the_iso_path_and_qa(engine: Engine, session: Session,
                                            settings: Settings,
                                            monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:  # fmt: skip
    job_id = queue(session)
    calls = fake_pipeline(monkeypatch, lambda plan, **kw: PipelineResult(
        iso_path=settings.output_dir / "iso" / "Filme.iso", qa_json='{"ok": 1}'))  # fmt: skip

    assert make_worker(engine, settings).run_once() is True

    done = job(engine, job_id)
    assert (done.status, done.iso_path, done.qa_json) == (
        "done", str(settings.output_dir / "iso" / "Filme.iso"), '{"ok": 1}')
    assert calls[0]["job_dir"] == settings.work_dir / "jobs" / str(job_id)
    assert calls[0]["plan"] == make_plan()


def test_progress_is_saved_at_most_once_a_second(engine: Engine, session: Session,
                                                 settings: Settings,
                                                 monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:  # fmt: skip
    job_id = queue(session)
    clock = [0.0]
    seen: list[float] = []

    def behaviour(plan: Any, report: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> PipelineResult:
        report(Step.VIDEO_PASS1, 0.1)
        seen.append(job(engine, job_id).progress)
        report(Step.VIDEO_PASS1, 0.2)  # same second: not saved
        seen.append(job(engine, job_id).progress)
        clock[0] = 1.5
        report(Step.VIDEO_PASS1, 0.3)
        seen.append(job(engine, job_id).progress)
        return PipelineResult(iso_path=Path("/x.iso"), qa_json=None)

    fake_pipeline(monkeypatch, behaviour)
    make_worker(engine, settings, clock).run_once()

    assert seen == pytest.approx([0.1 * 35 / 102, 0.1 * 35 / 102, 0.3 * 35 / 102])


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("error", "status", "message"),
    [
        (PipelineError("Falhou na etapa Áudio.", Step.AUDIO), "failed", "Falhou na etapa Áudio."),
        (RuntimeError("boom"), "failed", worker.UNEXPECTED),
    ],
)
def test_failures(engine: Engine, session: Session, settings: Settings,
                  monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, error: Exception, status: str,
                  message: str) -> None:  # fmt: skip
    job_id = queue(session)

    def behaviour(plan: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> PipelineResult:
        raise error

    fake_pipeline(monkeypatch, behaviour)
    make_worker(engine, settings).run_once()

    failed = job(engine, job_id)
    assert (failed.status, failed.error) == (status, message)
    log = settings.work_dir / "jobs" / str(job_id) / "job.log"
    if isinstance(error, RuntimeError):
        assert "Traceback" in log.read_text()


def test_qa_failure_keeps_the_report(engine: Engine, session: Session, settings: Settings,
                                     monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:  # fmt: skip
    job_id = queue(session)
    report = QaReport(checks=(Check(name="sync", label="Áudio sincronizado", ok=False),),
                      iso_bytes=1, estimated_bytes=1)  # fmt: skip

    def behaviour(plan: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> PipelineResult:
        raise QaFailed(report)

    fake_pipeline(monkeypatch, behaviour)
    make_worker(engine, settings).run_once()

    failed = job(engine, job_id)
    assert failed.status == "failed" and "Áudio sincronizado" in failed.error
    assert QaReport.model_validate_json(failed.qa_json) == report


def test_cancel_requested_by_the_user(engine: Engine, session: Session, settings: Settings,
                                      monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:  # fmt: skip
    job_id = queue(session)

    def behaviour(plan: Any, should_cancel: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> PipelineResult:
        store.request_cancel(engine, job_id)
        assert should_cancel()
        raise Canceled

    fake_pipeline(monkeypatch, behaviour)
    make_worker(engine, settings).run_once()

    assert job(engine, job_id).status == "canceled"


def test_sigterm_fails_the_running_job(engine: Engine, session: Session, settings: Settings,
                                       monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:  # fmt: skip
    job_id = queue(session)
    instance = make_worker(engine, settings)

    def behaviour(plan: Any, should_cancel: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> PipelineResult:
        instance.handle_signal(signal.SIGTERM, None)
        assert should_cancel()
        raise Canceled

    fake_pipeline(monkeypatch, behaviour)
    instance.run_once()

    failed = job(engine, job_id)
    assert (failed.status, failed.error) == ("failed", worker.STOPPED)


def test_recover_fails_running_jobs_and_removes_partial_isos(
    engine: Engine, session: Session, settings: Settings
) -> None:
    job_id = queue(session)
    store.claim_next(engine)
    make_file(settings.output_dir / "iso" / "Filme.iso.part")
    make_file(settings.output_dir / "iso" / "Outro.iso")

    make_worker(engine, settings).recover()

    assert (job(engine, job_id).status, job(engine, job_id).error) == ("failed", worker.RESTARTED)
    assert sorted(p.name for p in (settings.output_dir / "iso").iterdir()) == ["Outro.iso"]


def test_serve_waits_for_migrations_then_runs_until_stopped(
    engine: Engine, settings: Settings, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    instance = make_worker(engine, settings)
    answers = iter([False, True])
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "is_migrated", lambda engine: next(answers))
    runs: list[int] = []

    def run_once() -> bool:
        runs.append(1)
        if len(runs) == 2:
            instance.stop.set()
        return False

    monkeypatch.setattr(instance, "run_once", run_once)
    monkeypatch.setattr(instance, "recover", lambda: runs.append(0))

    worker.serve(instance, engine, poll_seconds=0.0)

    assert runs == [0, 1, 1]


def test_main_wires_signals_and_serves(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setenv("DATABASE_URL", f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'db.sqlite'}")
    handlers: dict[int, Any] = {}
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker.signal, "signal", lambda sig, handler: handlers.update({sig: handler}))
    served: list[Worker] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "serve", lambda instance, engine: served.append(instance))

    worker.main()

    assert set(handlers) == {signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT}
    assert len(served) == 1
```

Expected: FAIL with `ModuleNotFoundError: projeto_dvd.jobs.worker`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `src/projeto_dvd/jobs/worker.py`

```python
"""The worker service: one job at a time, claimed from SQLite, run by the pipeline.

Runs as `python -m projeto_dvd.jobs.worker` in its own compose service (init: true reaps
ffmpeg if anything goes wrong). It never migrates: it waits for the web to do it.
"""

import logging
import math
import signal
import threading
import time
import traceback
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

from sqlalchemy import Engine
from sqlmodel import Session

from projeto_dvd.config import Settings
from projeto_dvd.db.session import is_migrated, make_engine
from projeto_dvd.jobs import store
from projeto_dvd.jobs.pipeline import PipelineError, QaFailed, Step, overall_progress, run_pipeline
from projeto_dvd.jobs.runner import Canceled, ProcessRunner, Tools
from projeto_dvd.jobs.store import JobStatus
from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import DvdPlan

RESTARTED = "O worker reiniciou no meio da geração. Gere de novo."
STOPPED = "O worker foi parado (docker compose stop) no meio da geração. Gere de novo."
UNEXPECTED = "Erro inesperado na geração. Veja os detalhes técnicos no log."
SAVE_EVERY = 1.0
CANCEL_EVERY = 1.0

log = logging.getLogger("projeto_dvd.worker")


def default_tools(job_dir: Path, should_cancel: Callable[[], bool]) -> Tools:
    return ProcessRunner(log_path=job_dir / "job.log", should_cancel=should_cancel, cwd=job_dir)


@dataclass
class Worker:
    engine: Engine
    settings: Settings
    make_tools: Callable[[Path, Callable[[], bool]], Tools] = default_tools
    clock: Callable[[], float] = time.monotonic
    stop: threading.Event = field(default_factory=threading.Event)

    def handle_signal(self, signum: int, frame: object) -> None:
        # Only a flag: the running tool is stopped by the cancel check, then the job is saved.
        self.stop.set()

    def recover(self) -> None:
        """A job left running belongs to a worker that died; a .part file is half a copy."""
        failed = store.fail_interrupted(self.engine, RESTARTED)
        if failed:
            log.warning("marked %d interrupted job(s) as failed", failed)
        for part in (self.settings.output_dir / "iso").glob("*.iso.part"):
            part.unlink()

    def run_once(self) -> bool:
        job_id = store.claim_next(self.engine)
        if job_id is None:
            return False
        with Session(self.engine) as session:
            job = store.get_job(session, job_id)
            assert job is not None
            plan = DvdPlan.model_validate_json(job.plan_json)
        job_dir = self.settings.work_dir / "jobs" / str(job_id)
        job_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        should_cancel = self._cancel_check(job_id)
        report = self._reporter(job_id)
        try:
            result = run_pipeline(
                plan, media_dir=self.settings.media_dir, job_dir=job_dir,
                output_dir=self.settings.output_dir,
                tools=self.make_tools(job_dir, should_cancel), report=report,
                should_cancel=should_cancel,
            )  # fmt: skip
        except Canceled:
            if self.stop.is_set():
                store.finish(self.engine, job_id, JobStatus.FAILED, error=STOPPED)
            else:
                store.finish(self.engine, job_id, JobStatus.CANCELED)
        except QaFailed as error:
            store.finish(self.engine, job_id, JobStatus.FAILED, error=error.message,
                         qa_json=error.report.model_dump_json())  # fmt: skip
        except PipelineError as error:
            store.finish(self.engine, job_id, JobStatus.FAILED, error=error.message)
        except Exception:
            with (job_dir / "job.log").open("a") as job_log:
                job_log.write(traceback.format_exc())
            log.exception("job %d failed unexpectedly", job_id)
            store.finish(self.engine, job_id, JobStatus.FAILED, error=UNEXPECTED)
        else:
            store.finish(self.engine, job_id, JobStatus.DONE, qa_json=result.qa_json,
                         iso_path=str(result.iso_path))  # fmt: skip
        return True

    def _cancel_check(self, job_id: int) -> Callable[[], bool]:
        last, answer = [-math.inf], [False]

        def should_cancel() -> bool:
            if self.stop.is_set():
                return True
            now = self.clock()
            if now - last[0] >= CANCEL_EVERY:
                last[0] = now
                answer[0] = store.cancel_requested(self.engine, job_id)
            return answer[0]

        return should_cancel

    def _reporter(self, job_id: int) -> Callable[[Step, float], None]:
        last = [-math.inf]

        def report(step: Step, fraction: float) -> None:
            now = self.clock()
            if fraction >= 1 or now - last[0] >= SAVE_EVERY:
                last[0] = now
                store.save_progress(self.engine, job_id, step, overall_progress(step, fraction))

        return report


def serve(worker: Worker, engine: Engine, poll_seconds: float = 2.0) -> None:
    while not is_migrated(engine):
        if worker.stop.wait(poll_seconds):
            return
    worker.recover()
    while not worker.stop.is_set():
        if not worker.run_once():
            worker.stop.wait(poll_seconds)


def main() -> None:
    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format="%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s")
    settings = Settings()
    engine = make_engine(settings.database_url)
    worker = Worker(engine, settings)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, worker.handle_signal)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, worker.handle_signal)
    log.info("worker started")
    serve(worker, engine)
    engine.dispose()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

  The `Worker` reporter saves the first report immediately (`last = -inf`), so the progress test
  sees `0.1` saved on the first call. `ProcessRunner.terminate_seconds` is 3 s, below the
  30 s `stop_grace_period`.

`docker-compose.yml` (add, after `web`):

```yaml
  worker:
    build:
      context: .
      target: runtime
    command: ["python", "-m", "projeto_dvd.jobs.worker"]
    # Reaps ffmpeg if the worker dies; the worker stops its tool within 3 s on SIGTERM.
    init: true
    stop_grace_period: 30s
    # The image healthcheck probes the web port; the worker has none.
    healthcheck:
      disable: true
    volumes:
      - ${HOST_MEDIA_DIR:?set HOST_MEDIA_DIR in .env}:/media:ro
      - ${HOST_WORK_DIR:?set HOST_WORK_DIR in .env}:/work
      - ${HOST_OUTPUT_DIR:?set HOST_OUTPUT_DIR in .env}:/output
      - data:/data
```

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("headers", "allowed"),
    [
        ({}, True),  # not a browser: no CSRF possible
        ({"sec-fetch-site": "same-origin"}, True),
        ({"sec-fetch-site": "none"}, True),
        ({"sec-fetch-site": "same-site"}, False),  # another port of localhost
        ({"sec-fetch-site": "cross-site"}, False),
        ({"origin": "http://localhost:8000", "host": "localhost:8000"}, True),
        ({"origin": "http://evil.example", "host": "localhost:8000"}, False),
    ],
)
def test_same_origin(headers: dict[str, str], allowed: bool) -> None:
    assert same_origin(Headers(headers)) is allowed
```

`src/projeto_dvd/api/security.py`:

```python
"""Refuse state-changing requests that a page from another site could send.

The algorithm of Go's CrossOriginProtection: trust Sec-Fetch-Site when the browser sends it;
otherwise compare Origin with Host; with neither header the request did not come from a
browser, so it cannot be a cross-site forgery.
"""

from urllib.parse import urlsplit

from starlette.datastructures import Headers
from starlette.responses import PlainTextResponse
from starlette.types import ASGIApp, Receive, Scope, Send

SAFE_METHODS = frozenset({"GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"})


def same_origin(headers: Headers) -> bool:
    site = headers.get("sec-fetch-site")
    if site is not None:
        return site in ("same-origin", "none")
    origin = headers.get("origin")
    if origin is None:
        return True
    return urlsplit(origin).netloc == headers.get("host")


class SameOriginMiddleware:
    def __init__(self, app: ASGIApp) -> None:
        self.app = app

    async def __call__(self, scope: Scope, receive: Receive, send: Send) -> None:
        if (
            scope["type"] == "http"
            and scope["method"] not in SAFE_METHODS
            and not same_origin(Headers(scope=scope))
        ):
            response = PlainTextResponse("Pedido de outro site recusado.", status_code=403)
            await response(scope, receive, send)
            return
        await self.app(scope, receive, send)
```

```python
    app.add_middleware(SameOriginMiddleware)
```

```python
import os
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from projeto_dvd.jobs.files import clean_job_dir, job_dir, log_tail, temporary_bytes
from tests.tree import make_file


def test_clean_keeps_only_the_log(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    root = job_dir(tmp_path, 3)
    make_file(root / "job.log", b"log")
    make_file(root / "disc.iso", b"x" * 1000)
    make_file(root / "dvd" / "VIDEO_TS" / "VTS_01_1.VOB", b"y" * 500)
    outside = make_file(tmp_path / "outside.txt", b"keep")
    os.symlink(outside, root / "link")

    assert temporary_bytes(tmp_path, 3) == 1500
    assert clean_job_dir(tmp_path, 3) == 1500

    assert sorted(p.name for p in root.iterdir()) == ["job.log"]
    assert outside.read_bytes() == b"keep"


def test_missing_folder_has_nothing_to_clean(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    assert temporary_bytes(tmp_path, 9) == 0
    assert clean_job_dir(tmp_path, 9) == 0
    assert log_tail(tmp_path, 9) == ""


def test_log_tail(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    make_file(job_dir(tmp_path, 1) / "job.log", "\n".join(f"l{i}" for i in range(500)).encode())

    tail = log_tail(tmp_path, 1, lines=3)

    assert tail == "l497\nl498\nl499"


def test_job_dir_must_stay_inside_work(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        job_dir(tmp_path, -1)
```

```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlmodel import Session

from projeto_dvd.app import create_app
from projeto_dvd.authoring.dvd.qa import Check, QaReport
from projeto_dvd.config import Settings
from projeto_dvd.jobs import store
from projeto_dvd.jobs.files import job_dir
from projeto_dvd.jobs.store import JobStatus
from tests.client import client_for
from tests.plans import make_plan
from tests.tree import make_file

SAME = {"Sec-Fetch-Site": "same-origin"}


@pytest.fixture
def settings(tmp_path: Path, database_url: str) -> Settings:
    return Settings(media_dir=tmp_path / "media", work_dir=tmp_path / "work",
                    output_dir=tmp_path / "out", database_url=database_url)  # fmt: skip


@pytest.fixture
def client(settings: Settings) -> Iterator[TestClient]:
    with client_for(create_app(settings)) as client:
        yield client


def new_job(client: TestClient, folder: str = "Filme") -> int:
    engine = client.app.state.engine  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    with Session(engine) as session:
        return store.enqueue(session, make_plan(folder=folder))


def engine_of(client: TestClient) -> object:
    return client.app.state.engine  # type: ignore[attr-defined]


def test_queued_job_waiting_for_the_worker(client: TestClient) -> None:
    job_id = new_job(client)

    page = client.get(f"/jobs/{job_id}")

    assert page.status_code == 200
    assert "Na fila" in page.text
    assert 'hx-trigger="every 2s"' in page.text


def test_queued_behind_another_job(client: TestClient) -> None:
    first = new_job(client, "A")
    second = new_job(client, "B")
    store.claim_next(engine_of(client))  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    page = client.get(f"/jobs/{second}")

    assert f"Começa quando o job #{first} (pasta A) terminar." in page.text


def test_running_job_shows_steps_progress_and_cancel(client: TestClient) -> None:
    job_id = new_job(client)
    engine = engine_of(client)
    store.claim_next(engine)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    store.save_progress(engine, job_id, "video_pass2", 0.5)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    page = client.get(f"/jobs/{job_id}/status")

    assert "Vídeo: 2ª passada (codificação)" in page.text
    assert 'value="50"' in page.text and "50%" in page.text
    assert "Pode fechar esta página" in page.text
    assert 'hx-post="' in page.text and "/cancel" in page.text
    assert "<html" not in page.text


def test_cancel_a_queued_job(client: TestClient) -> None:
    job_id = new_job(client)

    response = client.post(f"/jobs/{job_id}/cancel", headers=SAME)

    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert "Cancelado por você" in response.text
    assert "Gerar de novo" in response.text
    assert 'hx-trigger="every 2s"' not in response.text


def test_cancel_a_running_job_shows_canceling(client: TestClient) -> None:
    job_id = new_job(client)
    store.claim_next(engine_of(client))  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    response = client.post(f"/jobs/{job_id}/cancel", headers=SAME)

    assert "Cancelando..." in response.text


def test_done_job_shows_the_iso_and_the_qa(client: TestClient, settings: Settings) -> None:
    job_id = new_job(client)
    engine = engine_of(client)
    store.claim_next(engine)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    report = QaReport(checks=(Check(name="size", label="Cabe num DVD-9", ok=True,
                                    detail="4 GB de 8,5 GB"),), iso_bytes=4_000_000_000,
                      estimated_bytes=4_100_000_000)  # fmt: skip
    store.finish(engine, job_id, JobStatus.DONE, qa_json=report.model_dump_json(),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
                 iso_path=str(settings.output_dir / "iso" / "Filme.iso"))  # fmt: skip

    page = client.get(f"/jobs/{job_id}")

    assert "Pronto! O ISO passou na conferência automática." in page.text
    assert "iso\\Filme.iso" in page.text
    assert "Cabe num DVD-9" in page.text
    assert "ImgBurn" in page.text


def test_failed_job_explains_and_offers_to_retry(client: TestClient) -> None:
    job_id = new_job(client)
    engine = engine_of(client)
    store.claim_next(engine)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    store.finish(engine, job_id, JobStatus.FAILED, error="Falhou na etapa Áudio.")  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    page = client.get(f"/jobs/{job_id}")

    assert "Falhou na etapa Áudio." in page.text
    assert "Gerar de novo" in page.text


def test_log_fragment(client: TestClient, settings: Settings) -> None:
    job_id = new_job(client)
    make_file(job_dir(settings.work_dir, job_id) / "job.log", b"$ ffmpeg -y\nfeito\n")

    response = client.get(f"/jobs/{job_id}/log")

    assert "feito" in response.text and "<html" not in response.text


def test_clean_refuses_an_active_job(client: TestClient) -> None:
    job_id = new_job(client)

    assert client.post(f"/jobs/{job_id}/clean", headers=SAME).status_code == 409


def test_clean_a_finished_job(client: TestClient, settings: Settings) -> None:
    job_id = new_job(client)
    store.request_cancel(engine_of(client), job_id)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    root = job_dir(settings.work_dir, job_id)
    make_file(root / "job.log")
    make_file(root / "video.m2v", b"x" * 2_000_000)

    page = client.get(f"/jobs/{job_id}")
    response = client.post(f"/jobs/{job_id}/clean", headers=SAME)

    assert "Apagar arquivos temporários (2 MB)" in page.text
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert sorted(p.name for p in root.iterdir()) == ["job.log"]


def test_unknown_job_is_404(client: TestClient) -> None:
    assert client.get("/jobs/999").status_code == 404
    assert client.post("/jobs/999/cancel", headers=SAME).status_code == 404


def test_job_list_and_nav(client: TestClient) -> None:
    new_job(client, "Senhor dos Aneis")

    page = client.get("/jobs")

    assert "Senhor dos Aneis" in page.text and "DVD-9" in page.text
    assert 'href="/jobs"' in client.get("/").text
```

`src/projeto_dvd/jobs/files.py`:

```python
"""The job folders in WORK_DIR/jobs/<id>: size, cleanup and the log."""

import os
import shutil
from collections import deque
from pathlib import Path

LOG = "job.log"


def job_dir(work_dir: Path, job_id: int) -> Path:
    if job_id < 0:
        raise ValueError("invalid job id")
    return work_dir / "jobs" / str(job_id)


def temporary_bytes(work_dir: Path, job_id: int) -> int:
    root = job_dir(work_dir, job_id)
    if not root.is_dir():
        return 0
    total = 0
    for dirpath, _, filenames in os.walk(root, followlinks=False):
        for name in filenames:
            path = Path(dirpath) / name
            if path != root / LOG and not path.is_symlink():
                total += path.stat().st_size
    return total


def clean_job_dir(work_dir: Path, job_id: int) -> int:
    """Delete everything but the log; links are removed, never followed."""
    root = job_dir(work_dir, job_id)
    if not root.is_dir():
        return 0
    if not root.resolve().is_relative_to((work_dir / "jobs").resolve()):
        raise ValueError("job folder outside the work folder")
    freed = temporary_bytes(work_dir, job_id)
    for entry in os.scandir(root):
        if entry.name == LOG:
            continue
        if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
            shutil.rmtree(entry.path)
        else:
            os.unlink(entry.path)
    return freed


def log_tail(work_dir: Path, job_id: int, lines: int = 200) -> str:
    path = job_dir(work_dir, job_id) / LOG
    if not path.is_file():
        return ""
    with path.open(errors="replace") as log:
        return "\n".join(line.rstrip("\n") for line in deque(log, maxlen=lines))
```

`formatting.py` (add):

```python
def format_ago(value: datetime | None, now: datetime) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return "?"
    minutes = int((now - value).total_seconds() // 60)
    if minutes < 1:
        return "agora"
    if minutes < 60:
        return f"há {minutes} min"
    if minutes < 24 * 60:
        return f"há {minutes // 60} h"
    days = minutes // (24 * 60)
    return f"há {days} dia{'s' if days != 1 else ''}"


def format_remaining(seconds: float | None) -> str:
    if seconds is None:
        return "calculando..."
    minutes = round(seconds / 60)
    if minutes < 1:
        return "menos de 1 min"
    if minutes < 60:
        return f"cerca de {minutes} min"
    return f"cerca de {minutes // 60}h{minutes % 60:02d}"
```

  (Times are shown relative on purpose: the container runs in UTC and has no time zone data,
  so "às 14:05" would be three hours off for the Vini.)

`src/projeto_dvd/api/jobs.py`:

```python
"""Job pages: progress while it runs, the result, the log, cancel and cleanup."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Literal

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse
from sqlmodel import Session

from projeto_dvd.api.deps import EngineDep, SessionDep, SettingsDep, templates
from projeto_dvd.authoring.dvd.qa import QaReport
from projeto_dvd.config import Settings
from projeto_dvd.db.models import Job
from projeto_dvd.jobs import store
from projeto_dvd.jobs.files import clean_job_dir, log_tail, temporary_bytes
from projeto_dvd.jobs.pipeline import STEP_LABELS, Step
from projeto_dvd.jobs.store import ACTIVE, JobStatus, as_utc
from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import DvdPlan
from projeto_dvd.planner.profiles import PROFILES
from projeto_dvd.planner.source import VideoMode

router = APIRouter()
WORKER_GRACE_SECONDS = 60
MIN_PROGRESS_FOR_ESTIMATE = 0.02


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StepView:
    label: str
    state: Literal["done", "current", "todo"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class JobView:
    job: Job
    plan: DvdPlan
    profile_name: str
    steps: tuple[StepView, ...]
    percent: int
    active: bool
    canceling: bool
    started: datetime | None
    elapsed_seconds: float | None
    remaining_seconds: float | None
    queue_ahead: Job | None
    waiting_worker: bool
    qa: QaReport | None
    temp_bytes: int
    now: datetime


def job_view(session: Session, settings: Settings, job: Job, now: datetime) -> JobView:
    assert job.id is not None
    plan = DvdPlan.model_validate_json(job.plan_json)
    started = as_utc(job.started_at)
    elapsed = (now - started).total_seconds() if started else None
    remaining = None
    if job.status == JobStatus.RUNNING and elapsed and job.progress >= MIN_PROGRESS_FOR_ESTIMATE:
        remaining = elapsed * (1 - job.progress) / job.progress
    running = store.running_job(session)
    created = as_utc(job.created_at)
    queued = job.status == JobStatus.QUEUED
    return JobView(
        job=job, plan=plan, profile_name=PROFILES[plan.profile].name,
        steps=_steps(plan, job), percent=int(job.progress * 100),
        active=job.status in ACTIVE, canceling=job.status == JobStatus.RUNNING
        and job.cancel_requested, started=started, elapsed_seconds=elapsed,
        remaining_seconds=remaining,
        queue_ahead=running if queued and running is not None else None,
        waiting_worker=queued and running is None and created is not None
        and (now - created).total_seconds() > WORKER_GRACE_SECONDS,
        qa=QaReport.model_validate_json(job.qa_json) if job.qa_json else None,
        temp_bytes=0 if job.status in ACTIVE else temporary_bytes(settings.work_dir, job.id),
        now=now,
    )  # fmt: skip


@router.get("/jobs", response_class=HTMLResponse, name="jobs")
def jobs_page(request: Request, session: SessionDep) -> HTMLResponse:
    jobs = [(job, DvdPlan.model_validate_json(job.plan_json)) for job in store.list_jobs(session)]
    context = {"jobs": jobs, "profiles": PROFILES, "now": _now(), "as_utc": as_utc}
    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, "jobs.html", context)


@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}", response_class=HTMLResponse, name="job_page")
def job_page(request: Request, job_id: int, session: SessionDep,
             settings: SettingsDep) -> HTMLResponse:  # fmt: skip
    view = job_view(session, settings, _job(session, job_id), _now())
    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, "job.html", {"view": view})


@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}/status", response_class=HTMLResponse, name="job_status")
def job_status(request: Request, job_id: int, session: SessionDep,
               settings: SettingsDep) -> HTMLResponse:  # fmt: skip
    view = job_view(session, settings, _job(session, job_id), _now())
    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, "_job_status.html", {"view": view})


@router.get("/jobs/{job_id}/log", response_class=HTMLResponse, name="job_log")
def job_log(request: Request, job_id: int, session: SessionDep,
            settings: SettingsDep) -> HTMLResponse:  # fmt: skip
    _job(session, job_id)
    context = {"log": log_tail(settings.work_dir, job_id)}
    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, "_job_log.html", context)


@router.post("/jobs/{job_id}/cancel", response_class=HTMLResponse, name="cancel_job")
def cancel_job(request: Request, job_id: int, session: SessionDep, settings: SettingsDep,
               engine: EngineDep) -> HTMLResponse:  # fmt: skip
    _job(session, job_id)
    store.request_cancel(engine, job_id)
    session.expire_all()
    return job_status(request, job_id, session, settings)


@router.post("/jobs/{job_id}/clean", response_class=HTMLResponse, name="clean_job")
def clean_job(request: Request, job_id: int, session: SessionDep,
              settings: SettingsDep) -> HTMLResponse:  # fmt: skip
    if _job(session, job_id).status in ACTIVE:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409)
    clean_job_dir(settings.work_dir, job_id)
    return job_status(request, job_id, session, settings)


def _job(session: Session, job_id: int) -> Job:
    job = store.get_job(session, job_id)
    if job is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404)
    return job


def _steps(plan: DvdPlan, job: Job) -> tuple[StepView, ...]:
    shown = [
        step for step in Step
        if not (step == Step.PULLDOWN and plan.mode != VideoMode.FILM)
        and not (step == Step.SUBTITLES and not plan.subtitles)
    ]  # fmt: skip
    if job.status == JobStatus.DONE:
        return tuple(StepView(STEP_LABELS[step], "done") for step in shown)
    current = shown.index(Step(job.step)) if job.step in {s.value for s in shown} else -1
    if job.status == JobStatus.QUEUED:
        current = -1
    return tuple(
        StepView(STEP_LABELS[step], "done" if i < current else "current" if i == current
                 else "todo")
        for i, step in enumerate(shown)
    )  # fmt: skip


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(UTC)
```

  Register `ago=format_ago`, `remaining=format_remaining` in `deps.templates`; include

```html
{% set job = view.job %}
<section id="job-status" class="job-status" aria-live="polite"
  {% if view.active %}hx-get="{{ url_for('job_status', job_id=job.id) }}"
  hx-trigger="every 2s" hx-swap="outerHTML"{% endif %}>
  {% if job.status == "queued" %}
    {% if view.queue_ahead %}
    <p class="banner">Na fila. Começa quando o job #{{ view.queue_ahead.id }} (pasta {{ view.queue_ahead.folder }}) terminar.</p>
    {% elif view.waiting_worker %}
    <p class="banner banner-warning">Aguardando o worker. Confira se o serviço está rodando
      (<code>docker compose ps</code>); para subir: <code>docker compose up -d worker</code>.</p>
    {% else %}
    <p class="banner">Na fila.</p>
    {% endif %}
  {% elif job.status == "running" %}
    <p class="progress-line"><progress max="100" value="{{ view.percent }}"></progress>
      <span class="numbers">{{ view.percent }}%</span></p>
    <p class="muted">Começou {{ view.started | ago(view.now) }} · faltam {{ view.remaining_seconds | remaining }}</p>
    <p>Pode fechar esta página. A geração continua e você pode voltar pela lista de Jobs.</p>
  {% elif job.status == "done" %}
    <p class="banner banner-ok"><strong>Pronto! O ISO passou na conferência automática.</strong></p>
    <p>O ISO está na pasta de saída: <code>iso\{{ view.plan.iso_name }}</code>
      {% if view.qa %}({{ view.qa.iso_bytes | size }}){% endif %}.</p>
    <p class="muted">Para gravar no Windows: abra o ImgBurn, "Write image file to disc", escolha
      o ISO e use velocidade baixa (4x ou 8x). Teste antes no VLC: Mídia, Abrir disco.</p>
  {% elif job.status == "canceled" %}
    <p class="banner">Cancelado por você. Nada foi gravado na pasta de saída.</p>
  {% else %}
    <p class="banner banner-error">{{ job.error or "A geração falhou." }}</p>
  {% endif %}

  <ol class="steps">
    {% for step in view.steps %}
    <li class="step step-{{ step.state }}">{{ step.label }}</li>
    {% endfor %}
  </ol>

  {% if view.qa %}
  <h2>Conferência automática</h2>
  <ul class="checks">
    {% for check in view.qa.checks %}
    <li class="{{ 'check-ok' if check.ok and not check.warning else 'check-warning' if check.ok else 'check-failed' }}">
      {{ check.label }}{% if check.detail %} <span class="muted">({{ check.detail }})</span>{% endif %}</li>
    {% endfor %}
  </ul>
  {% endif %}

  {% if job.status == "done" %}
  <h2>Antes de gravar, confira no VLC</h2>
  <ol class="checklist">
    <li>O filme começa sozinho ao abrir o disco.</li>
    <li>"Menu" e "Title" não travam nem tiram o filme do lugar.</li>
    <li>Os capítulos pulam com "próximo".</li>
    <li>Áudio e legenda trocam; a faixa e a legenda do início são as escolhidas.</li>
    <li>Panorâmicas sem tranco nem pente; cores e faixas pretas corretas.</li>
    <li>Legenda legível, em até 2 linhas.</li>
    <li>Áudio sincronizado com a boca no começo, no meio e no fim.</li>
    <li>No fim do filme o disco para, sem travar.</li>
  </ol>
  {% endif %}

  <p class="actions">
    {% if job.status == "running" and not view.canceling %}
    <button class="button secondary" type="button"
            hx-post="{{ url_for('cancel_job', job_id=job.id) }}" hx-target="#job-status"
            hx-swap="outerHTML"
            hx-confirm="Cancelar a geração? O que já foi feito se perde (ainda não dá para retomar).">Cancelar</button>
    {% elif job.status == "queued" %}
    <button class="button secondary" type="button"
            hx-post="{{ url_for('cancel_job', job_id=job.id) }}" hx-target="#job-status"
            hx-swap="outerHTML" hx-confirm="Tirar este job da fila?">Cancelar</button>
    {% elif view.canceling %}
    <span class="muted">Cancelando...</span>
    {% endif %}
    {% if job.status in ("failed", "canceled") %}
    <a class="button secondary" href="{{ url_for('dvd_form', name=job.folder | urlencode) }}">Gerar de novo</a>
    {% endif %}
    {% if not view.active and view.temp_bytes %}
    <button class="button secondary" type="button"
            hx-post="{{ url_for('clean_job', job_id=job.id) }}" hx-target="#job-status"
            hx-swap="outerHTML"
            hx-confirm="Apagar {{ view.temp_bytes | size }} de arquivos temporários deste job? O log fica.{% if job.status == 'done' %} O ISO na pasta de saída não é afetado.{% elif view.qa %} O ISO reprovado que ficou na pasta de trabalho também será apagado.{% endif %}">Apagar arquivos temporários ({{ view.temp_bytes | size }})</button>
    {% endif %}
  </p>
</section>
```

```python
def settings(tmp_path: Path, database_url: str) -> Settings:

    make_file(media / "Filme" / "Filme.mkv", b"x" * 100)
    make_file(media / "Dois" / "a.mkv")
    make_file(media / "Dois" / "b.mkv")
    (tmp_path / "work").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "out").mkdir()
    return Settings(media_dir=media, work_dir=tmp_path / "work", output_dir=tmp_path / "out",
                    database_url=database_url)  # fmt: skip


@pytest.fixture
def probe_data() -> dict[str, object]:
    return json.loads(json.dumps(MOVIE))  # a copy each test can change


@pytest.fixture
def client(settings: Settings, probe_data: dict[str, object],
           monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> Iterator[TestClient]:  # fmt: skip
    monkeypatch.setattr(library, "run_ffprobe",
                        lambda path: ProbeResult(json=json.dumps(probe_data), error=None))  # fmt: skip
    usage = shutil.disk_usage(settings.work_dir)
    monkeypatch.setattr(pipeline.shutil, "disk_usage",
                        lambda path: usage._replace(free=100 * 10**9))  # fmt: skip
    with client_for(create_app(settings)) as client:
        yield client


def scan(client: TestClient, name: str = "Filme") -> str:
    return client.post(f"/folders/{quote(name)}/scan", headers=SAME).text


def form_data(**changes: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    data: dict[str, object] = {"profile": "dvd9", "audio": ["1", "2"], "first_audio": "2",
                               "subtitle": ["s3"], "subtitle_on": "", "chapters": "file",
                               "minutes": "5"}  # fmt: skip
    data.update(changes)
    return data


def test_folder_offers_generate_after_the_scan(client: TestClient) -> None:
    body = scan(client)

    assert "Gerar DVD" in body
    assert 'href="/folders/Filme/dvd"' in body


def test_folder_with_two_videos_explains(client: TestClient) -> None:
    body = scan(client, "Dois")

    assert "Não dá para gerar o DVD desta pasta" in body
    assert "vídeo só" in body


def test_unscanned_folder_asks_for_a_scan(client: TestClient) -> None:
    assert "Escaneie a pasta antes de gerar o disco." in client.get("/folders/Filme").text


def test_form_shows_the_suggestions(client: TestClient) -> None:
    scan(client)

    page = client.get("/folders/Filme/dvd")

    assert page.status_code == 200
    assert "Entrada: 1080p, 23,976 quadros/s, 2h00" in page.text
    assert "faixas pretas" in page.text
    assert 'name="first_audio" value="2" checked' in page.text  # Portuguese plays first
    assert "copiar sem perda" in page.text and "converter para Dolby Digital 5.1" in page.text
    assert "Legenda em imagem (PGS): ainda não suportada" in page.text
    assert "sugerido" in page.text and "Ótima" in page.text and "Aceitável" in page.text


def test_form_of_a_refused_folder_goes_back(client: TestClient) -> None:
    scan(client, "Dois")

    response = client.get("/folders/Dois/dvd", follow_redirects=False)

    assert response.status_code == 303
    assert response.headers["location"] == "/folders/Dois"


def test_estimate_updates_the_cards(client: TestClient) -> None:
    scan(client)

    response = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd/estimate", headers=SAME,
                           data=form_data(audio=["1", "2"], profile="dvd5"))  # fmt: skip

    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert "<html" not in response.text
    assert "Baixa" in response.text or "Aceitável" in response.text
    assert 'value="dvd5" checked' in response.text


def test_create_queues_a_job_and_redirects(client: TestClient) -> None:
    scan(client)

    response = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers=SAME, data=form_data(),
                           follow_redirects=False)  # fmt: skip

    assert response.status_code == 303
    job_url = response.headers["location"]
    assert job_url.startswith("/jobs/")
    with Session(client.app.state.engine) as session:  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        job = store.get_job(session, int(job_url.rsplit("/", 1)[1]))
        assert job is not None
        plan = DvdPlan.model_validate_json(job.plan_json)
    assert [a.index for a in plan.audio] == [2, 1]
    assert plan.chapters == (0.0, 600.0)


def test_second_click_goes_to_the_same_job(client: TestClient) -> None:
    scan(client)
    first = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers=SAME, data=form_data(),
                        follow_redirects=False)  # fmt: skip

    again = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers=SAME, data=form_data(),
                        follow_redirects=False)  # fmt: skip
    form = client.get("/folders/Filme/dvd", follow_redirects=False)

    assert again.headers["location"] == first.headers["location"]
    assert form.headers["location"] == first.headers["location"]
    assert "Gerando DVD" in client.get("/folders/Filme").text
    assert "gerando DVD" in client.get("/").text


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("changes", "message"),
    [
        ({"audio": []}, "Escolha ao menos uma faixa de áudio."),
        ({"first_audio": "9"}, "A faixa que toca ao colocar o disco precisa estar marcada."),
        ({"subtitle_on": "s4"}, "A legenda ligada ao iniciar precisa estar marcada."),
        ({"chapters": "every", "minutes": "0"},
         "O intervalo dos capítulos precisa ser de 1 minuto ou mais."),
        ({"minutes": "abc"}, "Formulário inválido. Recarregue a página."),
    ],
)  # fmt: skip
def test_validation_errors_keep_the_form(client: TestClient, changes: dict[str, object],
                                         message: str) -> None:  # fmt: skip
    scan(client)

    response = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers=SAME, data=form_data(**changes))

    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert message in response.text
    assert 'aria-invalid="true"' in response.text or "Formulário inválido" in message


def test_low_quality_needs_the_checkbox(client: TestClient, probe_data: dict[str, object]) -> None:
    probe_data["format"] = {"duration": "36000"}
    scan(client)

    refused = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers=SAME, data=form_data())
    accepted = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers=SAME,
                           data=form_data(confirm_low="1"), follow_redirects=False)  # fmt: skip

    assert "Confirme que quer gerar com qualidade baixa." in refused.text
    assert "Entendo que a qualidade vai ficar baixa" in refused.text
    assert accepted.status_code == 303


def test_existing_iso_needs_confirmation(client: TestClient, settings: Settings) -> None:
    make_file(settings.output_dir / "iso" / "Filme.iso", b"x" * 1000)
    scan(client)

    page = client.get("/folders/Filme/dvd")
    refused = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers=SAME, data=form_data())
    accepted = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers=SAME,
                           data=form_data(replace_iso="1"), follow_redirects=False)  # fmt: skip

    assert "Substituir o ISO existente" in page.text
    assert pipeline.ISO_EXISTS_FORM in refused.text
    assert accepted.status_code == 303


def test_mpeg2_rip_is_classified_by_its_frames(client: TestClient, probe_data: dict[str, object],
                                               monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:  # fmt: skip
    streams = probe_data["streams"]
    assert isinstance(streams, list)
    streams[0].update(codec_name="mpeg2video", width=720, height=480, r_frame_rate="179/6",
                      display_aspect_ratio="16:9")  # fmt: skip
    frames = [{"repeat_pict": r, "interlaced_frame": 0, "top_field_first": 1}
              for r in (1, 0, 1, 0) * 30]  # fmt: skip
    calls: list[list[str]] = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(dvd, "run_capture",
                        lambda command: calls.append(command) or json.dumps({"frames": frames}))  # fmt: skip
    scan(client)

    client.get("/folders/Filme/dvd")
    client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd/estimate", headers=SAME, data=form_data())

    assert len(calls) == 1  # cached between the form and the estimate


def test_cross_site_create_is_refused(client: TestClient) -> None:
    scan(client)

    response = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers={"Sec-Fetch-Site": "cross-site"},
                           data=form_data())  # fmt: skip

    assert response.status_code == 403


def test_regenerate_link_points_to_the_form(client: TestClient) -> None:
    scan(client)
    location = client.post("/folders/Filme/dvd", headers=SAME, data=form_data(),
                           follow_redirects=False).headers["location"]  # fmt: skip
    client.post(location + "/cancel", headers=SAME)

    assert 'href="/folders/Filme/dvd"' in client.get(location).text
```
