Expected: FAIL (no route `/folders/{name}/dvd`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `src/projeto_dvd/api/dvd.py`

```python
"""The DVD form: suggestions, live capacity, validation and the job creation."""

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
from fastapi.responses import HTMLResponse, RedirectResponse, Response
from pydantic import ValidationError
from sqlmodel import Session
from starlette.datastructures import FormData

from projeto_dvd.api.deps import FolderDep, MediaDirDep, SessionDep, SettingsDep, templates
from projeto_dvd.config import Settings
from projeto_dvd.db.models import Job
from projeto_dvd.jobs import store
from projeto_dvd.jobs.pipeline import preflight
from projeto_dvd.jobs.store import JobStatus
from projeto_dvd.library.scan import FolderView, Status, folder_view
from projeto_dvd.planner.calculator import Estimate, estimate
from projeto_dvd.planner.disc_source import DiscSource, folder_reasons, prepare, run_capture
from projeto_dvd.planner.dvd_plan import (
    Choices,
    audio_kbps,
    audio_output,
    build_plan,
    plan_chapters,
    suggest_choices,
    validate_choices,
)
from projeto_dvd.planner.profiles import PROFILES
from projeto_dvd.planner.source import geometry

router = APIRouter()
INVALID_FORM = "Formulário inválido. Recarregue a página."
# Frame scans of MPEG-2 rips, by (path, size, mtime_ns): the form and the estimates reuse them.
_frames: dict[tuple[str, int, int], str] = {}


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DvdState:
    visible: bool
    reasons: tuple[str, ...]
    active: Job | None
    last_done: Job | None


def dvd_state(session: Session, view: FolderView) -> DvdState:
    latest = store.latest_job(session, view.name)
    return DvdState(
        visible=view.status != Status.EMPTY,
        reasons=folder_reasons(view),
        active=store.active_job(session, view.name),
        last_done=latest if latest and latest.status == JobStatus.DONE else None,
    )


def parse_choices(form: FormData) -> Choices | None:
    try:
        return Choices(
            profile=str(form.get("profile", "")),
            audio=tuple(int(str(value)) for value in form.getlist("audio")),
            first_audio=int(str(form.get("first_audio") or -1)),
            subtitles=tuple(str(value) for value in form.getlist("subtitle")),
            subtitle_on=str(form.get("subtitle_on") or "") or None,
            chapters=str(form.get("chapters", "every")),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            minutes=int(str(form.get("minutes") or 0)),
            confirm_low=form.get("confirm_low") == "1",
            replace_iso=form.get("replace_iso") == "1",
        )
    except (ValueError, ValidationError):
        return None


@router.get("/folders/{name}/dvd", response_class=HTMLResponse, name="dvd_form")
def dvd_form(request: Request, name: str, folder: FolderDep, session: SessionDep,
             media_dir: MediaDirDep, settings: SettingsDep) -> Response:  # fmt: skip
    active = store.active_job(session, name)
    if active is not None:
        return RedirectResponse(request.url_for("job_page", job_id=active.id), status_code=303)
    source = _source(session, media_dir, name, folder)
    if not isinstance(source, DiscSource):
        return RedirectResponse(request.url_for("folder", name=name), status_code=303)
    choices = suggest_choices(source.summary, source.options, _duration(source))
    return _render_form(request, settings, source, choices, errors=[])


@router.post("/folders/{name}/dvd/estimate", response_class=HTMLResponse, name="dvd_estimate")
async def dvd_estimate(request: Request, name: str, folder: FolderDep, session: SessionDep,
                       media_dir: MediaDirDep) -> Response:  # fmt: skip
    choices = parse_choices(await request.form())
    source = _source(session, media_dir, name, folder)
    if choices is None or not isinstance(source, DiscSource):
        return HTMLResponse(f'<p class="banner banner-error">{INVALID_FORM}</p>')
    context = _cards_context(source, choices)
    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, "_profile_cards.html", context)


@router.post("/folders/{name}/dvd", response_class=HTMLResponse, name="create_dvd")
async def create_dvd(request: Request, name: str, folder: FolderDep, session: SessionDep,
                     media_dir: MediaDirDep, settings: SettingsDep) -> Response:  # fmt: skip
    active = store.active_job(session, name)
    if active is not None:
        return RedirectResponse(request.url_for("job_page", job_id=active.id), status_code=303)
    source = _source(session, media_dir, name, folder)
    if not isinstance(source, DiscSource):
        return RedirectResponse(request.url_for("folder", name=name), status_code=303)
    choices = parse_choices(await request.form())
    if choices is None:
        default = suggest_choices(source.summary, source.options, _duration(source))
        return _render_form(request, settings, source, default, errors=[INVALID_FORM])
    errors = validate_choices(choices, source.summary, source.options)
    if not errors:
        plan = build_plan(
            folder=name, path=source.video.path, size=source.video.size,
            mtime_ns=source.video.mtime_ns, summary=source.summary, options=source.options,
            choices=choices, mode=source.mode, top_field_first=source.top_field_first,
        )  # fmt: skip
        errors = preflight(settings.work_dir, settings.output_dir, plan.estimated_bytes,
                           plan.iso_name, choices.replace_iso)  # fmt: skip
    if errors:
        return _render_form(request, settings, source, choices, errors)
    try:
        job_id = store.enqueue(session, plan)
    except store.ActiveJobError as error:
        job_id = error.job_id
    return RedirectResponse(request.url_for("job_page", job_id=job_id), status_code=303)


def _source(session: Session, media_dir: Path, name: str, folder: Path) -> DiscSource | tuple[str, ...]:
    view = folder_view(session, media_dir, name, folder)
    reasons = folder_reasons(view)
    if reasons or len(view.files) != 1 or view.files[0].summary is None:
        return reasons or ("",)
    file = view.files[0]
    key = (file.video.path, file.video.size, file.video.mtime_ns)

    def cached_capture(command: list[str]) -> str:
        if key not in _frames:
            _frames[key] = run_capture(command)
        return _frames[key]

    assert file.summary is not None
    return prepare(name, file.video, file.summary, cached_capture)


def _duration(source: DiscSource) -> float:
    return source.summary.duration or 0.0


def _cards_context(source: DiscSource, choices: Choices) -> dict[str, object]:
    duration = _duration(source)
    kbps = audio_kbps(source.summary, choices)
    estimates: list[Estimate] = [
        estimate(profile, duration, kbps, len(choices.subtitles)) for profile in PROFILES.values()
    ]
    suggested = suggest_choices(source.summary, source.options, duration).profile
    chosen = next((e for e in estimates if e.profile.key == choices.profile), None)
    return {"estimates": estimates, "choices": choices, "suggested": suggested, "chosen": chosen}


def _render_form(request: Request, settings: Settings, source: DiscSource, choices: Choices,
                 errors: list[str]) -> HTMLResponse:  # fmt: skip
    video = source.summary.video
    assert video is not None
    existing = settings.output_dir / "iso" / f"{source.folder}.iso"
    chapters = plan_chapters(source.summary.chapters, _duration(source), choices.chapters,
                             choices.minutes)  # fmt: skip
    context = {
        "source": source, "choices": choices, "errors": errors, "video": video,
        "shape": geometry(video), "outputs": {t.index: audio_output(t) for t in source.summary.audio},
        "chapters": chapters, "existing": existing if existing.exists() else None,
        **_cards_context(source, choices),
    }  # fmt: skip
    return templates.TemplateResponse(request, "dvd_form.html", context)
```

  In `library.py`, `folder_page` and `scan` pass `"dvd": dvd_state(session, view)` to the
  templates; `library_page` passes `"active": store.active_folders(session)`. Include
  `dvd.router` in `create_app`.

`_dvd_block.html` (included at the top of `_folder_body.html`, so a scan re-renders it):

```html
{% if dvd.visible %}
<section class="dvd-block" id="dvd">
  {% if dvd.active %}
  <p class="banner">Gerando DVD: {{ (dvd.active.progress * 100) | int }}%.
    <a href="{{ url_for('job_page', job_id=dvd.active.id) }}">Ver o job</a></p>
  {% elif dvd.reasons %}
  <div class="banner banner-warning">
    <strong>Não dá para gerar o DVD desta pasta</strong>
    <ul>{% for reason in dvd.reasons %}<li>{{ reason }}</li>{% endfor %}</ul>
  </div>
  {% else %}
  <a class="button primary" href="{{ url_for('dvd_form', name=folder.name | urlencode) }}">Gerar DVD</a>
  {% endif %}
  {% if dvd.last_done %}
  <p class="muted">Último ISO: <a href="{{ url_for('job_page', job_id=dvd.last_done.id) }}">job #{{ dvd.last_done.id }}</a></p>
  {% endif %}
</section>
{% endif %}
```

  `folder.html`: the "Escanear" button becomes `class="button secondary"` when
  `dvd.visible and not dvd.reasons`, otherwise `class="button primary"` (the existing tests
  look for the hx attributes, not the class). `library.html`: next to the status badge,
  `{% if folder.name in active %}<span class="badge">gerando DVD</span>{% endif %}`.
  `_job_status.html`: "Gerar de novo" points to `url_for('dvd_form', name=job.folder | urlencode)`.

`_profile_cards.html`:

```html
<div id="profiles" class="profiles">
  {% for item in estimates %}
  {% set p = item.profile %}
  {% set used = (item.size_bytes / p.capacity_bytes * 100) | round | int %}
  <label class="profile-card{% if choices.profile == p.key %} chosen{% endif %}">
    <input type="radio" name="profile" value="{{ p.key }}"{% if choices.profile == p.key %} checked{% endif %}{% if item.quality == "Não cabe" %} disabled{% endif %}>
    <span class="profile-name">{{ p.name }} · {{ p.description }}</span>
    {% if suggested == p.key %}<span class="badge">sugerido</span>{% endif %}
    <span class="quality quality-{{ {'Ótima': 'great', 'Aceitável': 'ok', 'Baixa': 'low', 'Não cabe': 'none'}[item.quality] }}">{{ item.quality }}</span>
    <span class="numbers">Usa {{ item.size_bytes | size }} de {{ p.capacity_bytes | size }}</span>
    <span class="bar"><span style="width: {{ [used, 100] | min }}%"></span></span>
    <span class="muted numbers">vídeo a {{ "%.1f" | format(item.video_kbps / 1000) | replace(".", ",") }} Mbps</span>
    {% if choices.profile == p.key %}<span class="chosen-label">Escolhido</span>{% endif %}
  </label>
  {% endfor %}
  <p class="muted help-line">A qualidade depende de quanto espaço sobra para a imagem. Ótima a partir de 5 Mbps.{% if suggested == "dvd9" %} A mídia de dupla camada é mais cara.{% endif %}</p>
  {% if chosen and chosen.quality == "Baixa" %}
  <label class="confirm"><input type="checkbox" name="confirm_low" value="1"{% if choices.confirm_low %} checked{% endif %}>
    Entendo que a qualidade vai ficar baixa e quero gerar mesmo assim</label>
  {% endif %}
</div>
```

`dvd_form.html`:

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% block title %}Gerar DVD · {{ source.folder }}{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<a class="back" href="{{ url_for('folder', name=source.folder | urlencode) }}">← {{ source.folder }}</a>
<h1>Gerar DVD de <span class="folder-name">{{ source.folder }}</span></h1>
<p class="muted">Entrada: {{ video | video_label | replace(" · H.264", "") | replace(" · ", ", ") }} quadros/s, {{ source.summary.duration | duration }}</p>
<p class="muted">Saída: DVD NTSC {{ shape.aspect }}{% if shape.height < 480 %} com faixas pretas (o filme é {{ video.aspect | aspect }}){% elif shape.width < 720 %} com faixas laterais{% endif %}. Leva cerca de 20 a 30 min.</p>
{% if errors %}
<div class="banner banner-error" role="alert"><ul>{% for error in errors %}<li>{{ error }}</li>{% endfor %}</ul></div>
{% endif %}
<form method="post" action="{{ url_for('create_dvd', name=source.folder | urlencode) }}" class="dvd-form">
<div hx-post="{{ url_for('dvd_estimate', name=source.folder | urlencode) }}" hx-trigger="change"
     hx-target="#profiles" hx-swap="outerHTML" hx-include="closest form">
  <fieldset>
    <legend>Áudio</legend>
    {% for track in source.summary.audio %}
    {% set out = outputs[track.index] %}
    <div class="track">
      <label><input type="checkbox" name="audio" value="{{ track.index }}"{% if track.index in choices.audio %} checked{% endif %}{% if errors and "áudio" in errors | join %} aria-invalid="true"{% endif %}>
        {{ track.language | language }} · {{ track | channels }} · {{ track.codec | audio_codec }}{% if track.title %} · {{ track.title }}{% endif %}{% if track.default %} <span class="badge">padrão no arquivo</span>{% endif %}</label>
      <span class="muted">{% if out.action == "copy" %}copiar sem perda{% else %}converter para Dolby Digital {{ "5.1" if out.channels == 6 else out.channels ~ " canais" if out.channels > 2 else "estéreo" if out.channels == 2 else "mono" }} ({{ out.kbps }} kbps){% endif %}</span>
      <label class="radio"><input type="radio" name="first_audio" value="{{ track.index }}"{% if track.index == choices.first_audio %} checked{% endif %}> Toca ao colocar o disco</label>
    </div>
    {% endfor %}
    <p class="muted">As outras faixas trocam pelo botão Áudio do controle.</p>
  </fieldset>
  <fieldset>
    <legend>Legendas</legend>
    {% for option in source.options %}
    <div class="track{% if not option.supported %} unsupported{% endif %}">
      {% if option.supported %}
      <label><input type="checkbox" name="subtitle" value="{{ option.key }}"{% if option.key in choices.subtitles %} checked{% endif %}>
        {{ option.language_tag | language }}{% if option.title %} · {{ option.title }}{% endif %}{% if option.forced %} <span class="badge">forçada</span>{% endif %}{% if option.default %} <span class="badge">padrão no arquivo</span>{% endif %}</label>
      <label class="radio"><input type="radio" name="subtitle_on" value="{{ option.key }}"{% if option.key == choices.subtitle_on %} checked{% endif %}{% if errors and "legenda ligada" in errors | join %} aria-invalid="true"{% endif %}> Ligada ao iniciar</label>
      {% else %}
      <span>{{ option.language_tag | language }}{% if option.title %} · {{ option.title }}{% endif %}</span>
      <span class="muted">{{ option.reason }}</span>
      {% endif %}
    </div>
    {% else %}
    <p class="muted">O vídeo não tem legendas. Para incluir uma, coloque um .srt ao lado dele e escaneie de novo.</p>
    {% endfor %}
    {% if source.options %}
    <label class="radio"><input type="radio" name="subtitle_on" value=""{% if choices.subtitle_on is none %} checked{% endif %}> Nenhuma (liga pelo botão Legenda do controle)</label>
    {% endif %}
  </fieldset>
  <fieldset>
    <legend>Capítulos</legend>
    <label class="radio"><input type="radio" name="chapters" value="file"{% if choices.chapters == "file" %} checked{% endif %}{% if source.summary.chapters | length < 2 %} disabled{% endif %}>
      Do arquivo ({{ source.summary.chapters | length }}){% if source.summary.chapters | length < 2 %} <span class="muted">o arquivo não tem capítulos</span>{% endif %}</label>
    <label class="radio"><input type="radio" name="chapters" value="every"{% if choices.chapters == "every" %} checked{% endif %}>
      A cada <input type="number" name="minutes" min="1" max="60" value="{{ choices.minutes }}" class="minutes"{% if errors and "capítulos" in errors | join %} aria-invalid="true"{% endif %}> minutos</label>
    {% if chapters.note %}<p class="muted">{{ chapters.note }}</p>{% endif %}
  </fieldset>
  <fieldset>
    <legend>Disco</legend>
    {% include "_profile_cards.html" %}
  </fieldset>
</div>
  {% if existing %}
  <label class="confirm"><input type="checkbox" name="replace_iso" value="1"{% if choices.replace_iso %} checked{% endif %}>
    Substituir o ISO existente ({{ existing.stat().st_size | size }})</label>
  {% endif %}
  <p class="actions"><button class="button primary" type="submit">Gerar DVD</button></p>
</form>
{% endblock %}
```

  Add the `audio_codec` filter to `formatting.py` (`ac3` → "Dolby Digital (AC-3)", `eac3` →
  "Dolby Digital Plus", `dts` → "DTS", `truehd` → "Dolby TrueHD", `aac` → "AAC", `flac` →
  "FLAC", `mp3` → "MP3", `pcm_*` → "PCM", otherwise upper case) with a parametrized test; and
  simplify the "Entrada" line with a dedicated filter `input_label(video)` if the `replace`
  chain above reads badly ("1080p, 23,976 quadros/s, 2h00" is what the test expects).

`app.css` (append):

```css
/* DVD form */
.dvd-form fieldset { margin: 16px 0; padding: 12px 16px; border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: var(--radius); }
.dvd-form legend { padding: 0 6px; font-weight: 600; }
.track { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 4px 16px; align-items: baseline; padding: 6px 0; }
.track.unsupported { color: var(--muted); }
.radio { white-space: nowrap; }
.minutes { width: 4em; }
.profiles { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr)); gap: 12px; }
.profile-card { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 4px; padding: 12px;
  background: var(--surface); border: 2px solid var(--border); border-radius: var(--radius);
  cursor: pointer; }
.profile-card.chosen { border-color: var(--text); }
.profile-card input { position: absolute; opacity: 0; }
.profile-card:focus-within { outline: 2px solid var(--accent); outline-offset: 2px; }
.profile-name { font-weight: 600; }
.quality { font-weight: 700; }
.quality-great::before { content: "● "; } .quality-great { color: var(--ok); }
.quality-ok::before { content: "◐ "; } .quality-ok { color: #d6c25a; }
.quality-low::before { content: "○ "; } .quality-low { color: var(--error); }
.quality-none { color: var(--muted); }
.bar { height: 6px; background: var(--border); border-radius: 3px; overflow: hidden; }
.bar > span { display: block; height: 100%; background: var(--muted); }
.chosen-label { font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text); }
.confirm { display: block; margin: 12px 0; }
.help-line { grid-column: 1 / -1; margin: 0; }
[aria-invalid="true"] { outline: 2px solid var(--error); }
.dvd-block { margin: 16px 0; }
```

## Group 5: CI, docs and the final QA

### Task 17: End-to-end smoke in CI, documentation and manual QA

**Files:**
- Modify: `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, `README.md`, `README.pt-BR.md`, `CLAUDE.md`, `.env.example`

- [ ] **Step 1: CI smoke from the screen to the ISO** (after "Smoke test", before the logs
  step; `timeout-minutes` of the `docker` job set from the slow-test time measured in Task 11
  plus 10 minutes, at least 30)

```yaml
      - name: Generate a DVD end to end (web + worker)
        run: |
          mkdir -p "/tmp/ci/media/Disco DVD"
          chmod 777 "/tmp/ci/media/Disco DVD"
          docker compose run --rm --no-deps -T -v "/tmp/ci/media/Disco DVD:/gen" \
            --entrypoint ffmpeg web -nostdin -v error \
            -f lavfi -i testsrc2=s=1280x720:r=24000/1001:d=3 -f lavfi -i sine=d=3 \
            -c:v libx264 -preset ultrafast -pix_fmt yuv420p -c:a aac \
            -metadata:s:a:0 language=por /gen/clip.mkv
          base=http://127.0.0.1:8000
          curl -fsS -X POST -H "Sec-Fetch-Site: same-origin" "$base/folders/Disco%20DVD/scan" -o /dev/null
          curl -fsS "$base/folders/Disco%20DVD/dvd" | grep -q "Gerar DVD"
          job="$(curl -fsS -o /dev/null -w '%{redirect_url}' -X POST \
            -H "Sec-Fetch-Site: same-origin" \
            -d profile=dvd5 -d audio=1 -d first_audio=1 -d chapters=every -d minutes=5 \
            -d subtitle_on= "$base/folders/Disco%20DVD/dvd")"
          echo "job: $job"
          test -n "$job"
          for _ in $(seq 90); do
            status="$(curl -fsS "$job/status")"
            if echo "$status" | grep -q "Pronto!"; then break; fi
            if echo "$status" | grep -qE "banner-error|Cancelado"; then echo "$status"; exit 1; fi
            sleep 2
          done
          echo "$status" | grep -q "Pronto!"
          iso="/tmp/ci/output/iso/Disco DVD.iso"
          test -s "$iso"
          test "$(stat -c %u "$iso")" -eq 1000
```

  Also update the tools count in the existing smoke (`length == 7`, done in Task 9) and add
  `docker compose ps worker` to the logs step (`docker compose logs web worker`).
